// ============================================================================ // SMART CACHE HELPER (Optimasi Performa) // ============================================================================ function getCachedOrFetch_(cacheKey, ttlSeconds, fetchFunction) { try { const cached = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(cacheKey); if (cached) { return JSON.parse(cached); } } catch (e) { // Abaikan error cache, lanjut ambil langsung dari spreadsheet } const freshData = fetchFunction(); try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(freshData), ttlSeconds || 300); } catch (e) { // Jika data terlalu besar untuk cache, abaikan } return freshData; } function invalidateCache_(keysArray) { try { if (Array.isArray(keysArray) && keysArray.length > 0) { CacheService.getScriptCache().removeAll(keysArray); } } catch (e) {} } DATABASE / HELPER // ============================================================================ // Cache objek Spreadsheet/Sheet/header di memori runtime. Ini hanya mengurangi // round-trip Apps Script; isi data transaksi tetap selalu dibaca dari sheet. let _dbRuntime = null; const _sheetRuntime = Object.create(null); const _headerRuntime = Object.create(null); function db() { if (!_dbRuntime) _dbRuntime = SpreadsheetApp.openById(CONFIG.SPREADSHEET_ID); return _dbRuntime; } function sheet(n) { if (_sheetRuntime[n]) return _sheetRuntime[n]; const s = db().getSheetByName(n); if (!s) throw new Error('Sheet database tidak ditemukan: ' + n); _sheetRuntime[n] = s; return s; } function headers(n) { if (_headerRuntime[n]) return _headerRuntime[n].slice(); const s = sheet(n); const lc = s.getLastColumn(); const h = lc ? s.getRange(1, 1, 1, lc).getValues()[0].map(String) : []; _headerRuntime[n] = h; return h.slice(); } function invalidateRuntimeSchemaCache_(n) { if (n) delete _headerRuntime[n]; else Object.keys(_headerRuntime).forEach(k => delete _headerRuntime[k]); } function records(n) { const s = sheet(n), lr = s.getLastRow(), lc = s.getLastColumn(); if (lr < 2 || !lc) return []; const h = headers(n), v = s.getRange(2, 1, lr - 1, lc).getValues(); return v.map(r => { const o = {}; h.forEach((x, i) => o[x] = r[i]); return o; }); } /* * DATA ACCESS CEPAT — tidak mengubah struktur tabel. * Query berdasarkan satu kolom membaca kolom kunci terlebih dahulu, * lalu hanya mengambil baris yang cocok. */ function recordsByValue(n, columnName, wantedValue) { const s = sheet(n), lr = s.getLastRow(), lc = s.getLastColumn(); if (lr < 2 || !lc) return []; const h = headers(n); const ci = h.indexOf(columnName); if (ci < 0) throw new Error('Kolom ' + columnName + ' tidak ditemukan pada sheet ' + n + '.'); const wanted = text(wantedValue); if (!wanted) return []; const keys = s.getRange(2, ci + 1, lr - 1, 1).getValues(); const rows = []; for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { if (text(keys[i][0]) === wanted) rows.push(i + 2); } if (!rows.length) return []; // Kelompokkan baris berurutan agar getRange dipanggil sesedikit mungkin. const ranges = []; let start = rows[0], prev = rows[0]; for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) { if (rows[i] === prev + 1) { prev = rows[i]; } else { ranges.push([start, prev]); start = prev = rows[i]; } } ranges.push([start, prev]); const out = []; ranges.forEach(pair => { const vals = s.getRange(pair[0], 1, pair[1] - pair[0] + 1, lc).getValues(); vals.forEach(r => { const o = {}; h.forEach((x, i) => o[x] = r[i]); out.push(o); }); }); return out; } function getCachedJson_(key) { try { const raw = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(key); return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } } function putCachedJson_(key, value, seconds) { try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(key, JSON.stringify(value), seconds || 300); } catch (e) { // Cache hanya akselerator; kegagalan cache tidak menggagalkan request. } } // V17.8 CLEAN READ CACHE — hanya untuk data referensi yang relatif stabil. function invalidateReferenceCaches_() { try { CacheService.getScriptCache().removeAll([ 'simpel_periode_spj_v1', 'simpel_sekolah_list_v1', 'simpel_sekolah_map_v3' ]); } catch (e) { // Cache hanya akselerator. } } function insert(n, d) { const h = headers(n); sheet(n).appendRow(h.map(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d, k) ? d[k] : '')); return { success: true, sheet: n }; } function id(p) { return p + '-' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyyMMddHHmmss') + '-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000); } function text(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); } function normalizeNpsn_(v) { // NPSN harus diperlakukan sebagai key teks 8 digit. // Menangani angka Google Sheet, format 20315823.0, apostrof, spasi, // NBSP, dan nilai yang terselip karakter non-digit tanpa mengubah schema. let s = text(v).replace(/\u00A0/g, '').trim(); if (!s) return ''; s = s.replace(/^'+/, ''); if (/^\d+\.0+$/.test(s)) s = s.replace(/\.0+$/, ''); const digits = s.replace(/\D/g, ''); if (digits.length === 8) return digits; return s; } function normalizeKodeBarang_(v) { return text(v).replace(/\s+/g, ''); } function upper(v) { return text(v).toUpperCase(); } function num(v) { if (typeof v === 'number') return v; let s = text(v).replace(/[^0-9,.-]/g, '').replace(/\.(?=\d{3}(?:\D|$))/g, '').replace(',', '.'); const n = Number(s); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; } function dateNorm(v) { if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) return v; const d = new Date(v); return isNaN(d) ? null : d; } /* ============================================================================ * V14.2 SCHEMA MIGRATION — ADDITIVE / NON-DESTRUCTIVE * ============================================================================ * Tujuan: * - Menyelaraskan spreadsheet V14.1 dengan kontrak backend V14.2. * - Tidak menghapus sheet/kolom/data. * - Kolom lama seperti versi_rkas dipertahankan sebagai legacy compatibility. * - Sheet rkas_import dibuat hanya jika belum ada. * ============================================================================ */ function prepareDatabaseV142(token) { requireRole(token, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const ss = db(); const report = { success: true, version: CONFIG.VERSION, schema_version: '14.2.2', created_sheets: [], added_columns: [], preserved_columns: [], rows_touched: 0, errors: [] }; function ensureSheetWithHeaders_(name, expected) { let sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, expected.length).setValues([expected]); report.created_sheets.push(name); return sh; } const lastCol = Math.max(sh.getLastColumn(), 1); const current = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(String); const missing = expected.filter(h => !current.includes(h)); if (missing.length) { sh.insertColumnsAfter(lastCol, missing.length); sh.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, missing.length).setValues([missing]); missing.forEach(h => report.added_columns.push({ sheet: name, column: h })); } current.filter(h => h && !expected.includes(h)).forEach(h => { report.preserved_columns.push({ sheet: name, column: h }); }); return sh; } Object.keys(EXPECTED_HEADERS).forEach(function(name) { try { ensureSheetWithHeaders_(name, EXPECTED_HEADERS[name]); } catch (e) { report.success = false; report.errors.push({ sheet: name, error: e.message }); } }); /* * Backfill metadata hanya untuk kolom baru yang aman diturunkan dari data lama. * Tidak membuat id_import fiktif dan tidak mengubah ID transaksi/RKAS. */ try { const sh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const h = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String); const rows = Math.max(sh.getLastRow() - 1, 0); if (rows > 0) { const idx = {}; h.forEach(function(k, i) { idx[k] = i; }); const values = sh.getRange(2, 1, rows, h.length).getValues(); let changed = 0; values.forEach(function(row) { if (idx.nama_belanja_asli !== undefined && !text(row[idx.nama_belanja_asli])) { row[idx.nama_belanja_asli] = text(row[idx.uraian]); changed++; } if (idx.status_mapping !== undefined && !text(row[idx.status_mapping])) { row[idx.status_mapping] = 'LEGACY'; changed++; } if (idx.catatan_mapping !== undefined && !text(row[idx.catatan_mapping])) { row[idx.catatan_mapping] = 'Record legacy sebelum V14.2; tidak berasal dari import V14.2.'; changed++; } /* id_import/file_rkas_id/nomor_baris_sumber sengaja tetap kosong. */ }); if (changed) { sh.getRange(2, 1, rows, h.length).setValues(values); report.rows_touched += rows; } } } catch (e) { report.success = false; report.errors.push({ sheet: SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, error: 'Backfill: ' + e.message }); } return report; } function getSchemaContractV142(token) { requireRole(token, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); return { success: true, version: CONFIG.VERSION, schema_version: '14.2.2', expected_headers: EXPECTED_HEADERS, legacy_compatible: true, rules: [ 'Tidak ada DROP/DELETE sheet.', 'versi_rkas dipertahankan sebagai compatibility field.', 'id_rkas historis tidak diregenerasi.', 'id_import hanya diisi oleh pipeline import baru.', 'rkas_import adalah staging, bukan pengganti master_rkas.' ] }; } function validateDatabaseStructure(t) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const r = { success: true, version: CONFIG.VERSION, schema_version: '14.2.2', sheets: [], errors: [] }; Object.keys(EXPECTED_HEADERS).forEach(n => { try { const h = headers(n), e = EXPECTED_HEADERS[n], missing = e.filter(x => !h.includes(x)), extra = h.filter(x => !e.includes(x)); r.sheets.push({ sheet: n, valid: !missing.length, missing, extra, rows: Math.max(sheet(n).getLastRow() - 1, 0) }); if (missing.length) { r.success = false; r.errors.push({ sheet: n, missing }); } } catch(err) { r.success = false; r.errors.push({ sheet: n, error: err.message }); } }); return r; } // ============================================================================ // AUTHENTICATION & SESSION // ============================================================================ function passwordSalt_() { return Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, '') + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, ''); } function passwordHash_(password, salt) { const bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, String(salt || '') + ':' + String(password || ''), Utilities.Charset.UTF_8); return bytes.map(function(b){ return (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join(''); } function verifyPassword_(password, hash, salt) { return !!hash && !!salt && passwordHash_(password, salt) === String(hash); } function ensureUserPasswordColumns_() { const ss = db(); let sh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS); if (!sh) throw new Error('Sheet users tidak ditemukan.'); let lastCol = Math.max(sh.getLastColumn(), 1); let h = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(String); const required = ['password', 'password_hash', 'password_salt']; const missing = required.filter(k => !h.includes(k)); if (missing.length) { sh.insertColumnsAfter(lastCol, missing.length); sh.getRange(1, lastCol + 1, 1, missing.length).setValues([missing]); h = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(String); } return {sheet: sh, headers: h}; } function writeUserPassword_(sh, row, password) { const h = headers(SHEETS.USERS); const saltCol = h.indexOf('password_salt') + 1; const hashCol = h.indexOf('password_hash') + 1; const passCol = h.indexOf('password') + 1; if (saltCol < 1 || hashCol < 1) { // Jangan pernah menghapus password lama bila kolom hash belum tersedia. return {migrated:false}; } const salt = passwordSalt_(); const hash = passwordHash_(password, salt); sh.getRange(row, saltCol).setValue(salt); sh.getRange(row, hashCol).setValue(hash); // Password legacy TETAP dipertahankan agar kompatibel dengan database lama // dan tidak pernah berubah menjadi kosong setelah login/migrasi. // Hash + salt menjadi mekanisme verifikasi utama; plaintext tetap tersedia // sebagai fallback kompatibilitas untuk akun lama. return {migrated:true, salt:salt, hash:hash, plaintextPreserved: passCol > 0}; } function login(username, password) { username = text(username); password = String(password || ''); if (!username || !password) return { success: false, message: 'Username dan password wajib diisi.' }; // Pastikan akun lama tetap dapat login dan kolom hash tersedia sebelum membaca data. const userMeta = ensureUserPasswordColumns_(); const sh = userMeta.sheet; const userList = records(SHEETS.USERS); const usernameKey = username.toLowerCase(); const u = userList.find(x => text(x.username).toLowerCase() === usernameKey); if (!u) return { success: false, message: 'Username atau password salah.' }; if (upper(u.status) !== 'AKTIF') return { success: false, message: 'Akun tidak aktif.' }; let valid = verifyPassword_(password, text(u.password_hash), text(u.password_salt)); // Kompatibilitas penuh akun lama: password plaintext tetap diterima sekali, // kemudian langsung dimigrasikan ke hash+salt tanpa memutus login. if (!valid && text(u.password)) { valid = String(u.password) === password; if (valid) { const row = userList.indexOf(u) + 2; const migrated = writeUserPassword_(sh, row, password); // Jika migrasi tidak dapat dilakukan, password plaintext tetap dipertahankan // agar akun tidak terkunci. } } if (!valid) return { success: false, message: 'Username atau password salah.' }; const role = upper(u.role); let npsnVal = ''; let namaSekolahVal = ''; let kecamatanVal = ''; let alamatLengkapVal = ''; if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) { npsnVal = text(u.npsn || u.username); try { const sekMap = getSekolahMap(); if (sekMap && sekMap[npsnVal]) { namaSekolahVal = sekMap[npsnVal].nama_sekolah || ''; kecamatanVal = sekMap[npsnVal].kecamatan || '-'; alamatLengkapVal = sekMap[npsnVal].alamat_lengkap || ''; } else { const sekRecord = records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH).find(s => text(s.npsn) === npsnVal); if (sekRecord) { namaSekolahVal = text(sekRecord.nama_sekolah || ''); kecamatanVal = text(sekRecord.kecamatan || '-'); alamatLengkapVal = text(sekRecord.alamat_lengkap || ''); } } } catch(err) { // Fallback aman jika tabel sekolah bermasalah namaSekolahVal = text(u.nama_pengguna || 'Satuan Pendidikan'); kecamatanVal = '-'; alamatLengkapVal = '-'; } } const session = { token: Utilities.getUuid(), id: u.id || '', username: u.username || '', nama_pengguna: u.nama_pengguna || '', role: role, npsn: npsnVal, nama_sekolah: namaSekolahVal, kecamatan: kecamatanVal, alamat_lengkap: alamatLengkapVal, login_at: new Date().toISOString(), last_activity_at: new Date().toISOString() }; try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put('SESSION_' + session.token, JSON.stringify(session), CONFIG.SESSION_SECONDS); updateLastLogin(u.id); } catch(e) {} return { success: true, token: session.token, user: session }; } function getSession(t) { if (!t) return null; const v = CacheService.getScriptCache().get('SESSION_' + t); if (!v) return null; try { return JSON.parse(v); } catch(e) { return null; } } function touchSession(t) { const s = getSession(t); if (!s) throw new Error('Sesi login tidak valid atau telah berakhir.'); const now = Date.now(); const loginAt = Date.parse(s.login_at || ''); const lastAt = Date.parse(s.last_activity_at || s.login_at || ''); if (loginAt && now - loginAt > CONFIG.SESSION_SECONDS * 1000) { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('SESSION_' + t); throw new Error('Sesi maksimum 6 jam telah berakhir. Silakan login kembali.'); } if (lastAt && now - lastAt > CONFIG.SESSION_IDLE_SECONDS * 1000) { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('SESSION_' + t); throw new Error('Sesi berakhir karena tidak ada aktivitas selama 30 menit. Silakan login kembali.'); } s.last_activity_at = new Date().toISOString(); CacheService.getScriptCache().put('SESSION_' + t, JSON.stringify(s), CONFIG.SESSION_SECONDS); return s; } function validateSession(t) { return touchSession(t); } function requireRole(t, roles) { const s = validateSession(t); if (!roles.includes(upper(s.role))) throw new Error('Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk fungsi ini.'); return s; } function logout(t) { if (t) CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('SESSION_' + t); return { success: true, message: 'Logout berhasil.' }; } function updateLastLogin(uid) { const s = sheet(SHEETS.USERS), h = headers(SHEETS.USERS), ic = h.indexOf('id') + 1, lc = h.indexOf('last_login') + 1; if (ic < 1 || lc < 1) return; for (let r = 2; r <= s.getLastRow(); r++) { if (String(s.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === String(uid)) { s.getRange(r, lc).setValue(new Date()); return; } } } function resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsn) { const s = validateSession(t), r = upper(s.role); if (r === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) return text(s.npsn || s.username); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN wajib ditentukan.'); if (r === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) { const pid = [s.id, s.username, s.pengawas].map(text).filter(Boolean); const ok = records(SHEETS.PENUGASAN_PENGAWAS).some(x => pid.includes(text(x.id_pengawas)) && text(x.npsn) === text(npsn) && upper(x.status) === 'AKTIF' ); if (!ok) throw new Error('Pengawas tidak memiliki penugasan untuk sekolah ini.'); } return text(npsn); } function getSekolahByUser(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]), x = records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH).find(a => text(a.npsn) === text(s.npsn || s.username)); if (!x) throw new Error('Data sekolah tidak ditemukan.'); return { success: true, data: x }; } // ============================================================================ // MASTER DATA & SEKOLAH / PENGAWAS MAP // ============================================================================ function _dedupeSystemMasterSPJ_() { // Bersihkan duplikasi master bawaan sistem tanpa menyentuh master kustom DINAS. const spjCodes = ['SPJ-01','SPJ-02','SPJ-03','SPJ-04','SPJ-05','SPJ-06','SPJ-07','SPJ-08','SPJ-09','SPJ-10']; const indicatorCodes = ['AUTO-RKAS','AUTO-DOKUMEN','AUTO-TRANSAKSI','AUTO-DUPLIKASI','AUTO-PAJAK','AUTO-KONSISTENSI']; const spjTargets = {}; spjCodes.forEach(k => spjTargets[k] = []); const indTargets = {}; indicatorCodes.forEach(k => indTargets[k] = []); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ), hs = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ); const spjCodeCol = hs.indexOf('kode_spj') + 1; if (spjCodeCol > 0) { const last = sh.getLastRow(); for (let r = last; r >= 2; r--) { const k = upper(sh.getRange(r, spjCodeCol).getValue()); if (spjTargets[k]) spjTargets[k].push(r); } } const shi = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_INDIKATOR), hi = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_INDIKATOR); const indCodeCol = hi.indexOf('kode_indikator') + 1; if (indCodeCol > 0) { const last = shi.getLastRow(); for (let r = last; r >= 2; r--) { const k = upper(shi.getRange(r, indCodeCol).getValue()); if (indTargets[k]) indTargets[k].push(r); } } Object.keys(spjTargets).forEach(k => { const rows = spjTargets[k].slice().sort((a,b) => a-b); for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) sh.deleteRow(rows[i]); }); Object.keys(indTargets).forEach(k => { const rows = indTargets[k].slice().sort((a,b) => a-b); for (let i = rows.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) shi.deleteRow(rows[i]); }); } function _ensureSystemMasterSPJ_() { _dedupeSystemMasterSPJ_(); const all = records(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ); let spj = all.find(x => text(x.id_spj) === 'SPJ-BKU' || upper(x.kode_spj) === 'SPJ-01' || upper(x.nama_spj).indexOf('BUKU KAS UMUM') >= 0); } function ensureSystemMasterSPJ(t) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const r = _ensureSystemMasterSPJ_(); return { success: true, data: r }; } function getMasterSPJ(t) { validateSession(t); _ensureSystemMasterSPJ_(); return ''; } function _frekuensiDariPeriode_(p) { const idp = upper(p && p.id_periode); const nama = upper(p && p.nama_periode); if (/PER-Q[1-4]-\d{4}/.test(idp) || /TRIWULAN|KUARTAL/.test(nama)) return 'TRIWULANAN'; } function simpanFrekuensiPenilaianSPJ(t, d) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); d=d||{}; const f=_normalisasiFrekuensiPenilaian_(d.frekuensi); const tahun=num(d.tahun)||new Date().getFullYear(); } function simpanMasterSPJ(t, d) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); d = d || {}; const idSpj = text(d.id_spj) || id('SPJ'); const kode = text(d.kode_spj); const nama = text(d.nama_spj); if (!kode || !nama) throw new Error('Kode SPJ dan nama SPJ wajib diisi.'); '' : num(d.bulan); const nama = text(d.nama_periode); if (!tahun || !nama) throw new Error('Tahun dan nama periode wajib diisi.'); if (bulan !== '' && (bulan < 1 || bulan > 12)) throw new Error('Bulan harus 1 sampai 12.'); const status = upper(d.status || 'AKTIF'); if (!['AKTIF','NONAKTIF'].includes(status)) throw new Error('Status periode tidak valid.'); const all=records(SHEETS.PERIODE_SPJ), dup=all.find(x=>text(x.nama_periode)===nama&&text(x.id_periode)!==idPeriode); if(dup) throw new Error('Nama periode sudah digunakan.'); const sh=sheet(SHEETS.PERIODE_SPJ),h=headers(SHEETS.PERIODE_SPJ),idc=h.indexOf('id_periode')+1; const data={id_periode:idPeriode,tahun:tahun,bulan:bulan,nama_periode:nama,tanggal_mulai:text(d.tanggal_mulai),tanggal_selesai:text(d.tanggal_selesai),status:status}; for(let r=2;r<=sh.getLastRow();r++) if(text(sh.getRange(r,idc).getValue())===idPeriode){sh.getRange(r,1,1,h.length).setValues([h.map(k=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(data,k)?data[k]:sh.getRange(r,h.indexOf(k)+1).getValue())]);return {success:true,updated:true,id_periode:idPeriode};} insert(SHEETS.PERIODE_SPJ,data); return {success:true,created:true,id_periode:idPeriode}; } function getSekolah(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const r = upper(s.role); // SEKOLAH: jangan scan seluruh sheet; ambil hanya NPSN milik sendiri. if (r === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) { const own = text(s.npsn || s.username); return { success: true, data: own ? recordsByValue(SHEETS.SEKOLAH, 'npsn', own) : [] }; } // DINAS & PENGAWAS: Manfaatkan getCachedOrFetch_ untuk manajemen cache yang lebih rapi const cacheKey = 'simpel_sekolah_list_v1'; const allResult = getCachedOrFetch_(cacheKey, 300, function() { return { success: true, data: records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH) }; }); // Jika yang meminta adalah DINAS, langsung kembalikan seluruh data if (r === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS) { return allResult; } // Jika yang meminta adalah PENGAWAS, saring dari hasil cache sekolah const ids = records(SHEETS.PENUGASAN_PENGAWAS) .filter(x => text(x.id_pengawas) === text(s.id) && upper(x.status) === 'AKTIF') .map(x => normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)) .filter(Boolean); if (!ids.length) return { success: true, data: [] }; const allowed = new Set(ids); const data = (allResult.data || []).filter(x => allowed.has(normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn))); return { success: true, data: data }; } /* ============================================================================ * RKAS REFERENCE RESOLVER — CANONICAL / READ-ONLY ENRICHMENT * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * Prinsip penting: * 1. referensi_barang hanya memperkaya tampilan/mapping. * 2. Tidak pernah mengubah pagu, volume, harga, status, atau jumlah baris RKAS. * 3. Exact kode rekening adalah prioritas utama. * 4. Legacy master_rkas yang id_referensi_barang-nya kosong tetap bisa terbaca. * 5. Cache key diversonkan agar perubahan referensi lama tidak tertahan cache. * ========================================================================== */ function _buildRkasReferenceIndex_() { const index = { byCode: {}, byName: {} }; let rows = []; try { rows = records(SHEETS.REFERENSI_BARANG); } catch (e) { return index; } rows.forEach(x => { // Mendukung struktur database baru: kode_barang/rincian_obyek, // sekaligus header legacy: Kode Barang/Rincian Objek. const kode = normalizeRkasCode_( x.kode_barang || x['Kode Barang'] || x['Kode barang'] || x['KODE BARANG'] || '' ); const nama = text( x.rincian_obyek || x['Rincian Objek'] || x['Rincian Obyek'] || x['Rincian Objek Belanja'] || x['RINCIAN OBJEK'] || '' ); if (!kode && !nama) return; const ref = { kode_barang: kode, kode_kelompok_belanja: kode, rincian_obyek: nama, nama_kelompok_belanja: nama }; if (kode && !index.byCode[kode]) index.byCode[kode] = ref; if (nama) { const nameKey = normalizeRkasText_(nama); if (nameKey && !index.byName[nameKey]) index.byName[nameKey] = ref; } }); return index; } function getRkasReferenceIndex_() { // V6 sengaja memutus cache V5 agar perubahan referensi_barang langsung terbaca. const cacheKey = 'simpel_rkas_ref_index_v6'; const cached = getCachedJson_(cacheKey); if (cached && cached.byCode && cached.byName) return cached; const index = _buildRkasReferenceIndex_(); putCachedJson_(cacheKey, index, 120); return index; } function getRkasMappingDiagnostic(t, kode) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const idx = _buildRkasReferenceIndex_(); const key = normalizeRkasCode_(kode); const ref = idx.byCode[key] || null; return { success: true, kode_input: kode || '', kode_normalized: key, matched: !!ref, reference: ref, total_reference_codes: Object.keys(idx.byCode || {}).length }; } function getReferensiBarangMap() { // Compatibility API lama: caller tetap menerima map keyed by kode. return getRkasReferenceIndex_().byCode || {}; } function resolveRkasReference_(row, refIndex) { const x = row || {}; const idx = refIndex || getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const byCode = idx.byCode || {}; const byName = idx.byName || {}; const codeCandidates = [ x.id_referensi_barang, x.kode_barang, x.kode_rekening ]; // 1) Exact code / id referensi. Ini sumber kebenaran utama. for (const candidate of codeCandidates) { const code = normalizeRkasCode_(candidate); if (code && byCode[code]) return Object.assign({ match_type: 'EXACT_CODE' }, byCode[code]); } // 2) Exact normalized name hanya untuk enrichment tampilan. // Tidak pernah mengubah data master atau pagu. const sourceName = text(x.nama_belanja_asli || x.nama_belanja || x.uraian || ''); const nameKey = normalizeRkasText_(sourceName); if (nameKey && byName[nameKey]) { return Object.assign({ match_type: 'EXACT_NAME' }, byName[nameKey]); } return null; } function enrichBelanjaMapping(row, refMapOrIndex) { const x = Object.assign({}, row || {}); const idx = refMapOrIndex && refMapOrIndex.byCode ? refMapOrIndex : { byCode: refMapOrIndex || {}, byName: {} }; const ref = resolveRkasReference_(x, idx); const sourceCode = normalizeRkasCode_(x.kode_barang || x.kode_rekening); // Jangan pernah menyentuh x.pagu/x.volume/x.harga_satuan/x.status_rkas. x.kode_barang = sourceCode; x.kode_kelompok_belanja = ref ? ref.kode_kelompok_belanja : (text(x.kode_kelompok_belanja) || sourceCode || 'LAINNYA'); x.nama_kelompok_belanja = ref ? ref.nama_kelompok_belanja : (text(x.nama_kelompok_belanja) || text(x.uraian) || sourceCode || 'Belanja Belum Terpetakan'); x.id_referensi_barang = text(x.id_referensi_barang || (ref ? ref.kode_barang : '')); x.nama_referensi = ref ? ref.rincian_obyek : ''; x.mapping_ref_status = ref ? ref.match_type : 'UNMAPPED'; return x; } function getSekolahMap() { const cacheKey = 'simpel_sekolah_map_v3'; const cached = getCachedJson_(cacheKey); if (cached) return cached; const map = {}; records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH).forEach(x => { const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn); if (!npsn) return; map[npsn] = { npsn: npsn, nama_sekolah: text(x.nama_sekolah), kecamatan: text(x.kecamatan || '-'), alamat_lengkap: text(x.alamat_lengkap || '-') }; }); putCachedJson_(cacheKey, map, 600); return map; } function invalidateRkasCache_(npsn) { try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove('simpel_rkas_npsn_v2_' + text(npsn)); } catch (e) { // Cache hanya akselerator. Kegagalan invalidasi tidak menggagalkan transaksi. } } function _dedupeRkasRowsForRead_(rows) { const seen = {}; const out = []; (rows || []).forEach(row => { // Identitas tampilan RKAS harus mengikuti isi item, bukan id_rkas. // Import ulang/versi berbeda dapat menghasilkan id_rkas berbeda untuk // rincian yang sebenarnya sama. Data transaksi tetap menggunakan id_rkas, // tetapi pembacaan daftar RKAS tidak boleh menampilkan item identik dua kali. const key = [ normalizeNpsn_(row.npsn), normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_rekening), normalizeRkasText_(row.uraian || row.nama_belanja_asli), normalizeRkasNumber_(row.volume), normalizeRkasText_(row.satuan), normalizeRkasNumber_(row.harga_satuan), normalizeRkasNumber_(row.pagu), text(row.id_referensi_barang) ].join('|'); if (seen[key]) return; seen[key] = true; out.push(row); }); return out; } function getRkasByNpsn_(npsn) { const key = 'simpel_rkas_npsn_v2_' + text(npsn); const cached = getCachedJson_(key); if (cached && Array.isArray(cached.data)) return cached.data; const raw = recordsByValue(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, 'npsn', npsn); const data = _dedupeRkasRowsForRead_(raw); // RKAS adalah data master yang relatif stabil; cache mengurangi scan berulang. putCachedJson_(key, {data: data}, 300); return data; } function getTransaksiByNpsn_(npsn) { const key = 'simpel_trx_npsn_v2_' + text(npsn); const cached = getCachedJson_(key); if (cached && Array.isArray(cached.data)) return cached.data; const data = recordsByValue(SHEETS.TRANSAKSI_SPJ, 'npsn', npsn); // Transaksi dapat berubah lebih sering, sehingga TTL dibuat lebih pendek. putCachedJson_(key, {data: data}, 60); return data; } function getPengawasMap() { const map = {}; records(SHEETS.USERS).forEach(x => { if (upper(x.role) === 'PENGAWAS') { map[text(x.username)] = text(x.nama_pengguna); map[text(x.id)] = text(x.nama_pengguna); } }); return map; } function getSekolahByNpsnPublic(npsn) { const key = text(npsn); if (!key) throw new Error('NPSN wajib diisi.'); const x = records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH).find(a => text(a.npsn) === key); if (!x) throw new Error('NPSN tidak ditemukan di database sekolah.'); return { success: true, data: { npsn: text(x.npsn), nama_sekolah: text(x.nama_sekolah), kecamatan: text(x.kecamatan || '-'), alamat_lengkap: text(x.alamat_lengkap || '-') } }; } function getSekolahPengelompokan(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS, CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS]); const allSchools = getSekolah(t).data || []; const assignments = getPenugasanPengawas(t).data || []; const byNpsn = {}; assignments.forEach(p => { byNpsn[text(p.npsn)] = p; }); const rows = allSchools.map(x => { const npsn = text(x.npsn); const p = byNpsn[npsn] || {}; return { npsn, nama_sekolah: text(x.nama_sekolah), kecamatan: text(x.kecamatan || '-'), alamat_lengkap: text(x.alamat_lengkap || '-'), id_pengawas: text(p.id_pengawas || ''), nama_pengawas: text(p.nama_pengawas || ''), status_penugasan: upper(p.status || '') || 'BELUM' }; }).sort((a,b) => String(a.kecamatan).localeCompare(String(b.kecamatan), 'id') || String(a.nama_sekolah).localeCompare(String(b.nama_sekolah), 'id') || String(a.npsn).localeCompare(String(b.npsn)) ); const groups = {}; rows.forEach(x => { const k = x.kecamatan || '-'; if (!groups[k]) groups[k] = { kecamatan: k, jumlah_sekolah: 0, sekolah: [] }; groups[k].jumlah_sekolah++; groups[k].sekolah.push(x); }); return { success: true, data: rows, groups: Object.keys(groups).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'id')).map(k=>groups[k]) }; } function normalizeRkasHeader_(v) { return text(v).replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'); } function parsePaguRkas_(v) { if (typeof v === 'number') return isFinite(v) ? Math.max(0, v) : 0; let raw = text(v); if (!raw) return 0; raw = raw.replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/rp/ig, '').replace(/\s/g, '').replace(/[^0-9,.-]/g, ''); // Format Indonesia: 177.650.000 if (/^-?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{3})+(?:,\d+)?$/.test(raw)) { raw = raw.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.'); } else if (raw.includes(',') && raw.includes('.')) { raw = raw.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.'); } else if (raw.includes(',') && !raw.includes('.')) { raw = raw.replace(',', '.'); } const n = Number(raw); return isFinite(n) ? Math.max(0, n) : 0; } function getPaguRkasAuditMap_() { const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const values = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); if (!values || values.length < 2) { return { map: {}, rows: 0, approvedRows: 0, matchedRows: 0, total: 0 }; } const h = values[0].map(normalizeRkasHeader_); const npsnCol = h.indexOf('npsn'); const paguCol = h.indexOf('pagu'); const statusCol = h.indexOf('status_rkas'); if (npsnCol < 0 || paguCol < 0) { throw new Error('Struktur master_rkas tidak sesuai: kolom npsn/pagu tidak ditemukan.'); } const map = {}; const rowCount = {}; let approvedRows = 0; let total = 0; for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) { const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(values[i][npsnCol]); if (!npsn) continue; const status = statusCol >= 0 ? upper(values[i][statusCol]) : 'APPROVED'; if (status !== 'APPROVED') continue; approvedRows++; const pagu = parsePaguRkas_(values[i][paguCol]); map[npsn] = (map[npsn] || 0) + pagu; rowCount[npsn] = (rowCount[npsn] || 0) + 1; total += pagu; } return { map, rowCount, rows: values.length - 1, approvedRows, matchedRows: Object.keys(rowCount).length, total }; } function getApprovedPaguMap_() { return getPaguRkasAuditMap_().map; } function getPaguPenugasanPengawas(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS, CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS]); const audit = getPaguRkasAuditMap_(); return { success: true, data: audit.map, meta: { source: SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, rows: audit.rows, approvedRows: audit.approvedRows, matchedNpsn: audit.matchedRows, totalPagu: audit.total } }; } function getPenugasanPengawas(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); let d = records(SHEETS.PENUGASAN_PENGAWAS); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) { const currentId = text(s.id); const currentUsername = text(s.username); const currentPengawasField = text(s.pengawas); d = d.filter(x => { const pid = text(x.id_pengawas); return pid === currentId || pid === currentUsername || pid === currentPengawasField; }); } const sekolahMap = getSekolahMap(); const pengawasMap = getPengawasMap(); const audit = getPaguRkasAuditMap_(); const enriched = d.map(x => { const npsnRaw = text(x.npsn); const npsnVal = normalizeNpsn_(npsnRaw); const sekInfo = sekolahMap[npsnVal] || {}; const pengawasId = text(x.id_pengawas); const matched = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(audit.map, npsnVal); const pagu = Number(audit.map[npsnVal] || 0); return Object.assign({}, x, { npsn: npsnVal || npsnRaw, npsn_raw: npsnRaw, npsn_key: npsnVal, pagu_rkas_match: matched, nama_sekolah: sekInfo.nama_sekolah || x.nama_sekolah || '-', kecamatan: sekInfo.kecamatan || '-', nama_pengawas: pengawasMap[pengawasId] || x.nama_pengawas || pengawasId, total_pagu: pagu, pagu_rkas: pagu, pagu_rkas_rows: Number(audit.rowCount[npsnVal] || 0) }); }); return { success: true, data: enriched, meta: { pagu_source: SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, rkas_rows: audit.rows, approved_rows: audit.approvedRows, matched_npsn: audit.matchedRows } }; } function getDiagnostikSumberPagu(t) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const ss = db(); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const values = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); const rawHeaders = values.length ? values[0].map(v => text(v)) : []; const headersNorm = rawHeaders.map(normalizeRkasHeader_); const statusCol = headersNorm.indexOf('status_rkas'); const npsnCol = headersNorm.indexOf('npsn'); const paguCol = headersNorm.indexOf('pagu'); const statusCounts = {}; const sampleNpsn = []; if (values.length > 1) { for (let i = 1; i < values.length; i++) { const st = statusCol >= 0 ? upper(values[i][statusCol]) : '(KOLOM STATUS TIDAK ADA)'; statusCounts[st] = (statusCounts[st] || 0) + 1; if (npsnCol >= 0 && sampleNpsn.length < 10) { const n = normalizeNpsn_(values[i][npsnCol]); if (n && !sampleNpsn.includes(n)) sampleNpsn.push(n); } } } return {success:true,data:{ spreadsheet_id:ss.getId(), spreadsheet_name:ss.getName(), master_rkas_sheet:sh.getName(), data_rows:Math.max(0, values.length - 1), last_column:sh.getLastColumn(), npsn_column_index:npsnCol + 1, pagu_column_index:paguCol + 1, status_column_index:statusCol + 1, status_counts_sample:statusCounts, sample_npsn:sampleNpsn }}; } function getDiagnostikMasterRKAS(t) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const ss = db(); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const values = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); const rawHeaders = values.length ? values[0].map(v => text(v)) : []; const headersNorm = rawHeaders.map(normalizeRkasHeader_); const statusCol = headersNorm.indexOf('status_rkas'); const npsnCol = headersNorm.indexOf('npsn'); const paguCol = headersNorm.indexOf('pagu'); const statusCounts = {}; const sampleNpsn = {}; if (values.length > 1) { for (let i=1; i= 0 ? upper(values[i][statusCol]) : '(KOLOM STATUS TIDAK ADA)'; statusCounts[st] = (statusCounts[st] || 0) + 1; if (npsnCol >= 0) { const k=normalizeNpsn_(values[i][npsnCol]); if(k && Object.keys(sampleNpsn).length<10) sampleNpsn[k]=true; } } } return {success:true,data:{spreadsheet_id:ss.getId(),spreadsheet_name:ss.getName(),sheet_name:sh.getName(),last_row:sh.getLastRow(),last_column:sh.getLastColumn(),data_rows:Math.max(0,values.length-1),headers:rawHeaders,headers_normalized:headersNorm,npsn_column:npsnCol+1,pagu_column:paguCol+1,status_column:statusCol+1,status_counts:statusCounts,sample_npsn:Object.keys(sampleNpsn)}}; } function getDiagnostikPaguPenugasan(t, npsn) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const audit = getPaguRkasAuditMap_(); const raw = text(npsn); const key = normalizeNpsn_(raw); const matched = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(audit.map, key); return { success: true, data: { npsn_raw: raw, npsn_key: key, matched, pagu: Number(audit.map[key] || 0), rkas_rows: Number(audit.rowCount[key] || 0), source: SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, total_rows: audit.rows, approved_rows: audit.approvedRows } }; } function cariGlobalSIMPEL(t, q) { const s = validateSession(t); const role = upper(s.role); const query = text(q).trim().toLowerCase(); if (query.length < 2) return { success: true, data: [], query: query }; // Pencarian ringan: hanya membaca tabel identitas, bukan transaksi/RKAS/dokumen. let sekolah = records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH); if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) { const own = text(s.npsn || s.username); sekolah = sekolah.filter(x => text(x.npsn) === own); } else if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) { const ids = records(SHEETS.PENUGASAN_PENGAWAS) .filter(x => text(x.id_pengawas) === text(s.id) && upper(x.status) === 'AKTIF') .map(x => text(x.npsn)); sekolah = sekolah.filter(x => ids.includes(text(x.npsn))); } const match = v => text(v).toLowerCase().includes(query); const hasil = []; sekolah.forEach(x => { if (match(x.npsn) || match(x.nama_sekolah) || match(x.kecamatan) || match(x.alamat_lengkap)) { hasil.push({ tipe: 'SEKOLAH', npsn: text(x.npsn), nama: text(x.nama_sekolah), sub: [text(x.kecamatan), text(x.alamat_lengkap)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') }); } }); // Nama lengkap pengguna/pengawas hanya dibuka untuk DINAS. if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS) { records(SHEETS.USERS).forEach(x => { if (upper(x.status) !== 'AKTIF') return; if (match(x.nama_pengguna) || match(x.username) || match(x.id) || match(x.nip)) { hasil.push({ tipe: 'PENGGUNA', id: text(x.id), username: text(x.username), nama: text(x.nama_pengguna), role: upper(x.role), sub: [upper(x.role), text(x.username), text(x.nip)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') }); } }); } hasil.sort((a,b) => { const aa = text(a.nama).toLowerCase(), bb = text(b.nama).toLowerCase(); const ar = aa === query ? 0 : aa.startsWith(query) ? 1 : 2; const br = bb === query ? 0 : bb.startsWith(query) ? 1 : 2; return ar - br || aa.localeCompare(bb, 'id'); }); return { success: true, data: hasil.slice(0, 25), query: query }; } // ============================================================================ // RKAS & REALISASI // ============================================================================ function getRKASAktif(t, npsn) { const s = validateSession(t); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) throw new Error('Pengawas tidak memiliki akses RKAS.'); npsn = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH ? text(s.npsn || s.username) : text(npsn); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN wajib ditentukan.'); return { success: true, npsn, data: _decorateRkasRows_(getRkasByNpsn_(npsn).filter(x => upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED')) }; } function getRKASHistory(t, npsn) { const s = validateSession(t); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) throw new Error('Pengawas tidak memiliki akses RKAS.'); npsn = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH ? text(s.npsn || s.username) : text(npsn); return { success: true, npsn, data: _decorateRkasRows_(getRkasByNpsn_(npsn)) }; } function _rkasImportIdFromVersion_(v) { return text(v); } function _decorateRkasRows_(rows) { return (rows || []).map(x => Object.assign({}, x, { versi_rkas: text(x.id_import) })); } function buatDraftRKAS(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const n = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (!n) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tidak ditemukan.'); const importId = id('IMP-RKAS'); const versi = 'V-' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyyMMddHHmmss'); insert(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT, { id_import: importId, npsn: n, nama_file: '', jenis_file: 'MANUAL', tahun: new Date().getFullYear(), versi_rkas: versi, tanggal_upload: new Date(), uploaded_by: text(s.username || s.id), file_id: '', status_import: 'DRAFT', total_baris: 0, berhasil_mapping: 0, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: 0, created_at: new Date() }); return { success: true, npsn: n, versi_rkas: importId, id_import: importId }; } function simpanDetailRKAS(t, d) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); d = d || {}; const n = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (normalizeNpsn_(d.npsn) !== n) throw new Error('NPSN tidak sesuai sesi.'); const importId = text(d.id_import || d.versi_rkas); if (!importId) throw new Error('ID import/versi RKAS wajib diisi.'); const imp = records(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT).find(x => text(x.id_import) === importId && normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === n); if (!imp) throw new Error('Import/versi RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); const approved = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).some(x => normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === n && text(x.id_import) === importId && upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED'); if (approved) throw new Error('RKAS APPROVED tidak boleh diubah.'); const data = Object.assign({}, d, { id_rkas: d.id_rkas || id('RKAS'), npsn: n, id_import: importId, id_referensi_barang: text(d.id_referensi_barang), nama_belanja_asli: text(d.nama_belanja_asli || d.nama_belanja || d.uraian), uraian: text(d.uraian || d.nama_belanja || d.nama_belanja_asli), volume: d.volume !== undefined ? num(d.volume) : '', harga_satuan: d.harga_satuan !== undefined ? num(d.harga_satuan) : '', pagu: d.pagu !== undefined ? num(d.pagu) : '', status_rkas: d.status_rkas || 'DRAFT', sumber_data: text(d.sumber_data || 'SEKOLAH'), update_at: new Date() }); delete data.versi_rkas; const result = insert(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, data); invalidateRkasCache_(n); return result; } function hapusRkasSekolah(t) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const n = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (!n) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tidak ditemukan.'); const rkasRows = getRkasByNpsn_(n); if (!rkasRows.length) { return { success: true, deleted: 0, npsn: n, message: 'Tidak ada RKAS sekolah yang perlu dihapus.' }; } // Safety guard: jangan hapus RKAS yang sudah dipakai transaksi/SPJ. const transaksi = records(SHEETS.TRANSAKSI_SPJ).filter(x => normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === n); if (transaksi.length) { const rkasIds = new Set(rkasRows.map(x => text(x.id_rkas)).filter(Boolean)); const rkasCodes = new Set(rkasRows.map(x => text(x.kode_rekening)).filter(Boolean)); const used = transaksi.filter(x => (text(x.id_rkas) && rkasIds.has(text(x.id_rkas))) || (text(x.kode_rekening) && rkasCodes.has(text(x.kode_rekening))) ); if (used.length) { throw new Error( 'RKAS tidak dapat dihapus karena sudah digunakan oleh ' + used.length + ' transaksi/SPJ. Hapus/selesaikan keterkaitan transaksi terlebih dahulu.' ); } } const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const nc = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1; if (nc < 1) throw new Error('Kolom npsn pada master_rkas tidak ditemukan.'); // Delete from bottom so row indexes remain valid. let deleted = 0; for (let r = sh.getLastRow(); r >= 2; r--) { if (normalizeNpsn_(sh.getRange(r, nc).getValue()) === n) { sh.deleteRow(r); deleted++; } } invalidateRkasCache_(n); return { success: true, npsn: n, deleted: deleted, message: deleted ? `Seluruh RKAS sekolah berhasil dihapus (${deleted} item).` : 'Tidak ada RKAS sekolah yang perlu dihapus.' }; } function hapusDetailRKAS(t, idRkas) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const n = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username), sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS), h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const ic = h.indexOf('id_rkas') + 1, nc = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1; for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === text(idRkas) && normalizeNpsn_(sh.getRange(r, nc).getValue()) === n) { sh.deleteRow(r); invalidateRkasCache_(n); return { success: true }; } } throw new Error('Detail RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); } function ajukanRKAS(t, versi) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const n = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username), importId = _rkasImportIdFromVersion_(versi); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS), h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const sc = h.indexOf('status_rkas') + 1, ic = h.indexOf('id_import') + 1, nc = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1, uc = h.indexOf('update_at') + 1; if (sc < 1 || ic < 1 || nc < 1) throw new Error('Struktur master_rkas belum sesuai schema baru.'); let count = 0; for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === importId && normalizeNpsn_(sh.getRange(r, nc).getValue()) === n) { sh.getRange(r, sc).setValue('DIAJUKAN'); if (uc > 0) sh.getRange(r, uc).setValue(new Date()); count++; } } if (!count) throw new Error('RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { status_import: 'DIAJUKAN', total_baris: count }); invalidateRkasCache_(n); return { success: true, count, id_import: importId, versi_rkas: importId }; } function reviewRKAS(t, versi, keputusan) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); keputusan = upper(keputusan); if (!['APPROVED', 'DITOLAK', 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN'].includes(keputusan)) throw new Error('Keputusan review tidak valid.'); const importId = _rkasImportIdFromVersion_(versi); return _decideRKASImport_(s, importId, keputusan, 'reviewRKAS'); } function _decideRKASImport_(s, importId, keputusan, sourceAction) { importId = text(importId); keputusan = upper(keputusan); if (!importId) throw new Error('ID import RKAS wajib diisi.'); if (!['APPROVED', 'DITOLAK', 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN'].includes(keputusan)) throw new Error('Keputusan review tidak valid.'); const imp = _findRkasImport_(importId); if (imp && text(imp.id_import_referensi)) { if (keputusan === 'APPROVED') { updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, {status_import:'APPROVED'}); return {success:true, npsn:normalizeNpsn_(imp.npsn), id_import:importId, keputusan:'APPROVED', alreadyApproved:true, id_import_referensi:text(imp.id_import_referensi)}; } throw new Error('Import ini merupakan salinan identik dari RKAS APPROVED dan tidak perlu diproses sebagai versi baru.'); } const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS), h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const ic = h.indexOf('id_import') + 1, sc = h.indexOf('status_rkas') + 1, nc = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1, uc = h.indexOf('update_at') + 1, pc = h.indexOf('tanggal_pengesahan') + 1, cc = h.indexOf('catatan_mapping') + 1; if (ic < 1 || sc < 1 || nc < 1) throw new Error('Schema master_rkas belum mendukung persetujuan RKAS.'); const matchedRows = []; for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === importId) matchedRows.push(r); } if (!matchedRows.length) throw new Error('RKAS/import tidak ditemukan: ' + importId); const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(sh.getRange(matchedRows[0], nc).getValue()); const previousStatus = upper(sh.getRange(matchedRows[0], sc).getValue()); if (previousStatus === 'APPROVED' && keputusan === 'APPROVED') { return { success:true, alreadyApproved:true, npsn:npsn, id_import:importId, keputusan:'APPROVED' }; } const now = new Date(); matchedRows.forEach(r => { sh.getRange(r, sc).setValue(keputusan); if (uc > 0) sh.getRange(r, uc).setValue(now); if (keputusan === 'APPROVED' && pc > 0) sh.getRange(r, pc).setValue(now); if (keputusan === 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN' && cc > 0) { const old = text(sh.getRange(r, cc).getValue()); sh.getRange(r, cc).setValue(old ? old + ' | Dikembalikan DINAS untuk perbaikan.' : 'Dikembalikan DINAS untuk perbaikan.'); } if (keputusan === 'DITOLAK' && cc > 0) { const old = text(sh.getRange(r, cc).getValue()); sh.getRange(r, cc).setValue(old ? old + ' | Ditolak DINAS.' : 'Ditolak DINAS.'); } }); // Jangan menonaktifkan APPROVED lain pada NPSN yang sama. // Satu sekolah boleh memiliki beberapa paket RKAS APPROVED yang valid // (misalnya versi/perubahan/import yang berbeda). Menu Review Dinas // menampilkan seluruh paket tersebut; histori tidak disembunyikan. updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { status_import: keputusan, total_baris: matchedRows.length, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: keputusan === 'DITOLAK' ? matchedRows.length : 0 }); invalidateRkasCache_(npsn); return { success: true, npsn: npsn, id_import: importId, versi_rkas: importId, keputusan: keputusan, previous_status: previousStatus, jumlah_baris: matchedRows.length, source_action: sourceAction || 'reviewRKAS' }; } function reviewRKASImport(t, payload) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload = payload || {}; return _decideRKASImport_(s, text(payload.id_import || payload.versi_rkas), upper(payload.keputusan), 'reviewRKASImport'); } function getRKASApprovalQueue(t, status) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const target = upper(status || 'DIAJUKAN'); const allowed = ['DIAJUKAN','APPROVED','PERLU_PERBAIKAN','DITOLAK']; if (!allowed.includes(target)) throw new Error('Status antrean RKAS tidak valid.'); const allImports = records(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT); const allMaster = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const schoolMap = getSekolahMap(); const result = []; // Paket IMPORT nyata: DIAJUKAN/APPROVED dari rkas_import. allImports.forEach(imp => { const importId = text(imp.id_import); if (!importId) return; const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(imp.npsn); if (!npsn) return; const importStatus = upper(imp.status_import || ''); const resolved = _rkasDetailRowsForImport_(allMaster, importId, allImports); const rows = resolved.rows || []; let effectiveStatus = importStatus; const rowStatuses = rows.map(x => upper(x.status_rkas)).filter(Boolean); if (!['APPROVED','DITOLAK','PERLU_PERBAIKAN','DIAJUKAN'].includes(effectiveStatus)) { if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'APPROVED')) effectiveStatus = 'APPROVED'; else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'DITOLAK')) effectiveStatus = 'DITOLAK'; else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN')) effectiveStatus = 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN'; else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'DIAJUKAN')) effectiveStatus = 'DIAJUKAN'; } if (effectiveStatus !== target) return; if (!rows.length) return; const totalPagu = rows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+num(x.pagu),0); if (target === 'APPROVED' && totalPagu <= 0) return; const school = schoolMap[npsn] || {}; result.push(Object.assign({}, imp, { id_import_paket: importId, npsn, nama_sekolah: text(school.nama_sekolah || ''), kecamatan: text(school.kecamatan || ''), alamat_lengkap: text(school.alamat_lengkap || ''), jumlah_baris: rows.length, total_baris: rows.length, total_pagu: totalPagu, status_import: effectiveStatus, status_rkas: effectiveStatus, _resolved_import_id: resolved.resolvedImportId, _referenced: resolved.referenced, _queue_source: 'IMPORT' })); }); // RKAS APPROVED lama/legacy: sumbernya langsung master_rkas, bukan rkas_import. // Hanya ditampilkan jika NPSN ada di master tabel sekolah resmi dan id_import kosong. // Pengelompokan paket: NPSN + versi_rkas + sumber_data. Tidak mengubah data lama. if (target === 'APPROVED') { const legacyMap = {}; allMaster.forEach(row => { const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(row.npsn); if (!npsn || !schoolMap[npsn]) return; if (text(row.id_import)) return; if (upper(row.status_rkas) !== 'APPROVED') return; const versi = text(row.versi_rkas || ''); const sumber = text(row.sumber_data || 'DATA-RKAS'); const key = [npsn, versi, sumber].join('||'); if (!legacyMap[key]) { legacyMap[key] = { id_import: '', id_import_paket: '', npsn, nama_sekolah: text(schoolMap[npsn].nama_sekolah || ''), kecamatan: text(schoolMap[npsn].kecamatan || ''), alamat_lengkap: text(schoolMap[npsn].alamat_lengkap || ''), nama_file: 'RKAS tersimpan (legacy)', jenis_file: 'LEGACY', tahun: '', versi_rkas: versi, sumber_data: sumber, status_import: 'APPROVED', status_rkas: 'APPROVED', jumlah_baris: 0, total_baris: 0, total_pagu: 0, tanggal_upload: row.tanggal_pengesahan || row.update_at || '', _queue_source: 'LEGACY' }; } legacyMap[key].jumlah_baris++; legacyMap[key].total_baris++; legacyMap[key].total_pagu += num(row.pagu); }); Object.keys(legacyMap).forEach(k => { const pkg = legacyMap[k]; if (pkg.jumlah_baris > 0 && pkg.total_pagu > 0) result.push(pkg); }); } // SEMUA paket valid dikembalikan. Tidak ada list[0] dan tidak ada pembatasan 1 paket per NPSN. result.sort((a,b)=>{ const sa = String(a.nama_sekolah || a.npsn || ''); const sb = String(b.nama_sekolah || b.npsn || ''); return sa.localeCompare(sb, 'id', {sensitivity:'base'}) || String(a.versi_rkas || '').localeCompare(String(b.versi_rkas || '')); }); return {success:true, status:target, data:result}; } // Detail RKAS legacy yang belum mempunyai id_import. Hanya baca; tidak mengubah data. function getRKASLegacyPackage(t, payload) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload = payload || {}; const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn); const versi = text(payload.versi_rkas || ''); const sumber = text(payload.sumber_data || ''); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN paket legacy wajib diisi.'); const school = getSekolahMap()[npsn]; if (!school) throw new Error('NPSN bukan sekolah resmi pada tabel sekolah.'); const rows = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).filter(x => normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === npsn && !text(x.id_import) && upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED' && text(x.versi_rkas || '') === versi && (!sumber || text(x.sumber_data || '') === sumber) ); if (!rows.length) throw new Error('Detail RKAS legacy APPROVED tidak ditemukan.'); const refIndex = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const enrichedRows = rows.map(row => enrichBelanjaMapping(row, refIndex)); return { success: true, source: 'LEGACY', npsn, nama_sekolah: text(school.nama_sekolah || ''), data: enrichedRows, summary: { jumlah_baris: enrichedRows.length, total_pagu: enrichedRows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+num(x.pagu),0) } }; } function getRKASByStatus(t, status, npsn) { const s = validateSession(t); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) throw new Error('Pengawas tidak memiliki akses RKAS.'); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) npsn = s.npsn || s.username; let d = npsn ? getRkasByNpsn_(npsn).filter(x => upper(x.status_rkas) === upper(status)) : records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).filter(x => upper(x.status_rkas) === upper(status)); return { success: true, data: d }; } function getRingkasanRKAS(t, npsn) { const d = getRKASHistory(t, npsn).data; const c = {}; d.forEach(x => c[upper(x.status_rkas)] = (c[upper(x.status_rkas)] || 0) + 1); return { success: true, npsn: getRKASHistory(t, npsn).npsn, ringkasan: c }; } function getSemuaUsulanRKAS(t) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); // Jangan kirim RKAS mentah. Setiap baris diperkaya dari master sekolah // dan referensi barang agar frontend selalu memiliki identitas sekolah // dan kelompok belanja yang benar. const sekolahMap = getSekolahMap(); const refIndex = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const d = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const enriched = d.map(row => { const x = Object.assign({}, row); const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn); const sekolah = sekolahMap[npsn] || {}; x.npsn = npsn; x.versi_rkas = text(x.id_import || ''); x.nama_sekolah = text(sekolah.nama_sekolah || ''); x.kecamatan = text(sekolah.kecamatan || ''); x.alamat_lengkap = text(sekolah.alamat_lengkap || ''); // Mapping hanya enrichment. Nilai pagu dan baris sumber tidak disentuh. const enrichedRow = enrichBelanjaMapping(x, refIndex); x.kode_barang = enrichedRow.kode_barang; x.kode_kelompok_belanja = enrichedRow.kode_kelompok_belanja; x.nama_kelompok_belanja = enrichedRow.nama_kelompok_belanja; x.id_referensi_barang = enrichedRow.id_referensi_barang; x.nama_referensi = enrichedRow.nama_referensi; x.mapping_ref_status = enrichedRow.mapping_ref_status; return x; }); return { success: true, data: enriched }; } function getRealisasiRKAS(t, npsn) { npsn = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsn); // Hanya membaca RKAS milik NPSN yang diminta. const rkasList = getRkasByNpsn_(npsn) .filter(x => upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED'); // Hanya membaca transaksi milik NPSN yang diminta. const transaksi = _canonicalBkuRowsAll_().filter(function(x){return normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===npsn;}); const refIndex = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const sekolahInfo = getSekolahMap()[npsn] || {}; // Indeks transaksi sekali. Sebelumnya setiap RKAS melakukan filter() // terhadap seluruh transaksi sekolah. const byIdRkas = {}; const byKodeRekening = {}; transaksi.forEach(tr => { const nilai = _bkuBudgetAmount_(tr); if (!(nilai > 0)) return; const idRkas = text(tr.id_rkas); const kode = text(tr.kode_rekening); if (idRkas) byIdRkas[idRkas] = (byIdRkas[idRkas] || 0) + nilai; if (kode) byKodeRekening[kode] = (byKodeRekening[kode] || 0) + nilai; }); const data = rkasList.map(r => { const idRkas = text(r.id_rkas); const kode = text(r.kode_rekening); const pagu = num(r.pagu); const totalRealisasi = idRkas && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byIdRkas, idRkas) ? byIdRkas[idRkas] : (byKodeRekening[kode] || 0); const sisaPagu = pagu - totalRealisasi; let status = 'NORMAL'; if (totalRealisasi > pagu) status = 'REALISASI_MELEBIHI_PAGU'; else if (sisaPagu === 0) status = 'HABIS'; return enrichBelanjaMapping(Object.assign({}, r, { npsn: npsn, nama_sekolah: text(sekolahInfo.nama_sekolah || ''), kecamatan: text(sekolahInfo.kecamatan || '-'), alamat_lengkap: text(sekolahInfo.alamat_lengkap || '-'), total_realisasi: totalRealisasi, sisa_pagu: sisaPagu, status: status }), refIndex); }); return { success: true, npsn: npsn, data: data }; } // ============================================================================ // DOKUMEN SPJ & TRANSAKSI // ============================================================================ function getDokumenAktifSPJ(npsn, periode, idSpj) { const d = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ).filter(x => text(x.npsn) === text(npsn) && text(x.id_periode) === text(periode) && text(x.id_spj) === text(idSpj) && upper(x.status) !== 'NONAKTIF'); d.sort((a, b) => num(b.versi) - num(a.versi)); return d[0] || null; } function getDokumenSPJ(t, idPeriode, npsnOpt) { const s = validateSession(t); let n = ''; if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) n = text(s.npsn || s.username); else if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS || s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS) { n = npsnOpt ? resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsnOpt) : ''; } // Hanya kirim dokumen aktif. Versi lama memang dipertahankan di database // untuk audit trail, tetapi tidak boleh ikut tampil di viewer pemeriksaan. let d = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ).filter(function(x) { return text(x.id_periode) === text(idPeriode) && upper(x.status || 'SUDAH_UPLOAD') !== 'NONAKTIF'; }); if (n) d = d.filter(function(x) { return text(x.npsn) === n; }); // Proteksi tambahan untuk data lama yang mungkin pernah menghasilkan // referensi ganda ke file fisik yang sama. Pertahankan versi tertinggi. const unique = {}; d.forEach(function(x) { const fileKey = text(x.file_id); const key = fileKey ? ('FILE|' + fileKey + '|SPJ|' + text(x.id_spj) + '|TX|' + text(x.id_transaksi)) : ('DOC|' + text(x.id_dokumen)); if (!key) return; if (!unique[key] || num(x.versi) > num(unique[key].versi)) unique[key] = x; }); d = Object.keys(unique).map(function(k) { return unique[k]; }); d.sort(function(a, b) { const ua = new Date(a.uploaded_at || 0).getTime() || 0; const ub = new Date(b.uploaded_at || 0).getTime() || 0; if (ub !== ua) return ub - ua; return num(b.versi) - num(a.versi); }); return { success: true, npsn: n, data: d }; } function getDokumenPdfBase64(t, fileId, idPeriode, npsnOpt) { const session = validateSession(t); const fid = text(fileId); if (!fid) throw new Error('file_id PDF wajib diisi.'); if (![CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS, CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH].includes(session.role)) throw new Error('Anda tidak berwenang membaca PDF ini.'); let npsn = ''; if (session.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) npsn = text(session.npsn || session.username); else if (npsnOpt) npsn = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsnOpt); // PDF hanya boleh dibaca jika file_id memang tercatat sebagai dokumen SPJ // pada sekolah/periode yang sedang diminta. Ini mencegah endpoint menjadi // proxy Drive umum yang dapat membaca file arbitrary. const docs = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ).filter(function(d) { return text(d.file_id) === fid && (!idPeriode || text(d.id_periode) === text(idPeriode)) && (!npsn || text(d.npsn) === npsn) && upper(d.status || 'SUDAH_UPLOAD') !== 'NONAKTIF'; }); if (!docs.length) throw new Error('Dokumen PDF tidak ditemukan atau Anda tidak memiliki hak akses.'); let file; try { file = DriveApp.getFileById(fid); } catch (e) { throw new Error('File PDF tidak dapat diakses oleh server aplikasi.'); } const blob = file.getBlob(); const mime = text(blob.getContentType()) || 'application/pdf'; if (mime !== 'application/pdf' && !/pdf/i.test(text(file.getName()))) throw new Error('File yang dipilih bukan PDF.'); return { success: true, file_id: fid, nama_file: file.getName(), mimeType: 'application/pdf', base64: Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes()) }; } function _assertOcrDocumentAccess_(t, fileId, idPeriode, npsnOpt) { const session = validateSession(t); const fid = text(fileId); if (!fid) throw new Error('file_id PDF wajib diisi.'); if ([CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS, CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH].indexOf(session.role) < 0) { throw new Error('Anda tidak berwenang melakukan OCR dokumen ini.'); } let npsn = ''; if (session.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) npsn = text(session.npsn || session.username); else if (npsnOpt) npsn = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsnOpt); const docs = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ).filter(function(d) { return text(d.file_id) === fid && (!idPeriode || text(d.id_periode) === text(idPeriode)) && (!npsn || text(d.npsn) === npsn) && upper(d.status || 'SUDAH_UPLOAD') !== 'NONAKTIF'; }); if (!docs.length) throw new Error('Dokumen PDF tidak ditemukan atau Anda tidak memiliki hak akses.'); return { session: session, npsn: npsn, document: docs[0] }; } function getOcrCapability(t) { validateSession(t); const available = typeof Drive !== 'undefined' && !!Drive.Files && typeof Drive.Files.create === 'function'; return { success: true, available: available, engine: available ? 'DRIVE_API_V3' : 'NONE', message: available ? 'OCR server tersedia.' : 'Advanced Google Service Drive API belum tersedia pada deployment ini.' }; } function ocrPdfPageImage(t, payload) { payload = payload || {}; const fileId = text(payload.fileId || payload.file_id); const idPeriode = text(payload.idPeriode || payload.id_periode); const npsn = text(payload.npsn); const pageNo = Math.max(1, Math.floor(num(payload.pageNo || payload.page || 1))); _assertOcrDocumentAccess_(t, fileId, idPeriode, npsn); if (typeof Drive === 'undefined' || !Drive.Files || !Drive.Files.create) { throw new Error('OCR belum aktif. Tambahkan Advanced Google Service Drive API pada project Apps Script.'); } const b64 = text(payload.imageBase64 || payload.base64).replace(/^data:image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+;base64,/, ''); if (!b64) throw new Error('Gambar halaman untuk OCR kosong.'); // Batasi payload hasil render agar satu halaman tidak membebani Apps Script. if (b64.length > 8 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('Gambar halaman terlalu besar untuk OCR. Kurangi resolusi render.'); const mimeType = /^image\/(png|jpeg|jpg)$/i.test(text(payload.mimeType)) ? text(payload.mimeType) : 'image/jpeg'; let tempId = ''; try { const bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(b64); const blob = Utilities.newBlob(bytes, mimeType, 'OCR_PAGE_' + pageNo + (mimeType.indexOf('png') >= 0 ? '.png' : '.jpg')); const resource = { name: 'OCR_PAGE_TMP_' + pageNo + '_' + new Date().getTime(), mimeType: 'application/vnd.google-apps.document' }; const temp = Drive.Files.create(resource, blob, { ocrLanguage: 'id', fields: 'id,name,mimeType' }); tempId = text(temp && temp.id); if (!tempId) throw new Error('Google Drive tidak mengembalikan ID hasil OCR halaman.'); let doc = null, lastErr = null; for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) { try { doc = DocumentApp.openById(tempId); if (doc) break; } catch (e) { lastErr = e; Utilities.sleep(350 * (attempt + 1)); } } if (!doc) throw (lastErr || new Error('Dokumen hasil OCR halaman belum dapat dibuka.')); const textOcr = text(doc.getBody().getText()).trim(); return { success: true, file_id: fileId, page: pageNo, text: textOcr, characters: textOcr.length, source: 'GOOGLE_DRIVE_PAGE_OCR_V3' }; } catch (e) { throw new Error('OCR halaman ' + pageNo + ' gagal: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e)); } finally { if (tempId) { try { Drive.Files.remove(tempId); } catch (_) {} } } } function ocrDokumenPdf(t, fileId, idPeriode, npsnOpt) { const meta = getDokumenPdfBase64(t, fileId, idPeriode, npsnOpt); // Advanced Drive Service pada Apps Script saat ini memakai Drive API v3. // V3 menggunakan Drive.Files.create(), bukan Drive.Files.insert() (v2). if (typeof Drive === 'undefined' || !Drive.Files || !Drive.Files.create) { throw new Error('OCR belum aktif. Di Apps Script buka Services (+) → tambahkan Drive API. Pastikan service Drive tersedia, lalu deploy ulang Web App.'); } let tempId = ''; try { const bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(meta.base64); const blob = Utilities.newBlob(bytes, 'application/pdf', meta.nama_file || 'dokumen.pdf'); // PDF diimpor sebagai Google Docs. Proses import Google Drive akan // menjalankan OCR; ocrLanguage hanya memberi hint bahasa Indonesia. const resource = { name: 'OCR_TMP_' + new Date().getTime(), mimeType: 'application/vnd.google-apps.document' }; const temp = Drive.Files.create(resource, blob, { ocrLanguage: 'id', fields: 'id,name,mimeType' }); tempId = text(temp && temp.id); if (!tempId) throw new Error('Google Drive tidak mengembalikan ID dokumen hasil OCR.'); // Konversi PDF → Google Docs kadang membutuhkan waktu singkat sebelum // DocumentApp dapat membukanya. Retry kecil mencegah false error. let doc = null; let lastErr = null; for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++) { try { doc = DocumentApp.openById(tempId); if (doc) break; } catch (openErr) { lastErr = openErr; Utilities.sleep(400 * (attempt + 1)); } } if (!doc) throw (lastErr || new Error('Dokumen hasil OCR belum dapat dibuka.')); const textOcr = text(doc.getBody().getText()); return { success: true, file_id: String(fileId), nama_file: meta.nama_file, text: textOcr, characters: textOcr.length, source: 'GOOGLE_DRIVE_OCR_V3' }; } catch (e) { throw new Error('OCR PDF gagal: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e)); } finally { if (tempId) { try { Drive.Files.remove(tempId); } catch (_) {} } } } function simpanDokumenSPJ(t, d) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]), n = text(s.npsn || s.username); if (text(d.npsn) !== n) throw new Error('NPSN tidak sesuai.'); if (!text(d.id_periode) || !text(d.id_spj)) throw new Error('Data dokumen belum lengkap.'); const all = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ), versi = all.filter(x => text(x.npsn) === n && text(x.id_periode) === text(d.id_periode) && text(x.id_spj) === text(d.id_spj)).reduce((m, x) => Math.max(m, num(x.versi)), 0) + 1; const sh = sheet(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ), h = headers(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ), sc = h.indexOf('status') + 1, nc = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1, pc = h.indexOf('id_periode') + 1, ic = h.indexOf('id_spj') + 1; for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r, nc).getValue()) === n && text(sh.getRange(r, pc).getValue()) === text(d.id_periode) && text(sh.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === text(d.id_spj)) { sh.getRange(r, sc).setValue('NONAKTIF'); } } return insert(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ, Object.assign({}, d, { id_dokumen: id('DOC'), npsn: n, versi, status: 'SUDAH_UPLOAD', uploaded_at: new Date() })); } function setStatusDokumenSPJNA(t, idPeriode, idSpj, alasan) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]), n = text(s.npsn || s.username), m = records(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ).find(x => text(x.id_spj) === text(idSpj)); } function getStatusSPJBulanan(t, idPeriode, npsn) { npsn = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, npsn); const master = records(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ).filter(x => upper(x.status_aktif) === 'AKTIF').sort((a, b) => num(a.urutan) - num(b.urutan)); 'BELUM_LENGKAP' : 'DALAM_PEMERIKSAAN' }; } // ============================================================================ // IMPORT BKU V6 — FORMAT NYATA BKU RKAS 2026 // Tidak mengubah struktur sheet. Excel -> transaksi_spj, PDF -> dokumen_spj. // ============================================================================ function _bkuMonthName_(m) { return ['Januari','Februari','Maret','April','Mei','Juni','Juli','Agustus','September','Oktober','November','Desember'][m - 1] || ('Bulan ' + m); } function _bkuDate_(v) { if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) return new Date(v.getFullYear(), v.getMonth(), v.getDate()); const s = text(v); let m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); if (m) return new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])); m = s.match(/^(\d{2})-(\d{2})-(\d{4})$/); if (m) return new Date(Number(m[3]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[1])); const d = new Date(s); return isNaN(d) ? null : new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()); } function _bkuPeriod_(year, month) { const f = _normalisasiFrekuensiPenilaian_(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPJ_FREKUENSI_PENILAIAN') || ''); if (!f) throw new Error('DINAS belum menentukan frekuensi penilaian SPJ. } function _extractBkuImportId_(row){ const s=text(row&&row.status_crosscheck); const m=s.match(/^BKU_EXCEL\|([^|]+)\|/i); return m?text(m[1]):''; } function _latestBkuImportForPeriod_(npsn,period){ const n=normalizeNpsn_(npsn),p=text(period); }) }; } function _canonicalRowsFromImportDetail_(npsn,period,imp){ if(!imp||!text(imp.id_import))return []; const n=normalizeNpsn_(npsn),p=text(period),importId=text(imp.id_import); const details=_bkuDetailRecordsForImport_(importId).filter(function(x){ return normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===n && text(x.id_periode)===p; '1' : '2') + '-' + year; const found = ctx.byId[idPeriode]; if (!found) throw new Error('Periode BKU tidak ditemukan: ' + idPeriode); return found; } function importBkuBundle(t, payload) { payload = payload || {}; const session = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(session.npsn || session.username); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tidak ditemukan pada session.'); if (payload.npsn && normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn) !== npsn) throw new Error('NPSN BKU tidak sesuai dengan sekolah yang sedang login.'); if (upper(payload.source_type || 'EXCEL_BKU_ARKAS') !== 'EXCEL_BKU_ARKAS') throw new Error('V21 hanya menerima Excel BKU ARKAS sebagai sumber BKU penilaian.'); const sourceRows = Array.isArray(payload.transactions) ? payload.transactions : []; if (!sourceRows.length) throw new Error('Tidak ada transaksi BKU yang dapat diimpor.'); if (sourceRows.length > 3000) throw new Error('Jumlah transaksi melebihi batas impor 3.000 baris per berkas.'); const importId='BKUIMP-'+new Date().getTime()+'-'+Math.floor(1000+Math.random()*9000); const sekolah = getSekolahMap()[npsn] || {}; const rkasMap = _bkuRkasIndex_(npsn); const periodMap = {}; const normalized = []; const warnings = []; let totalPenerimaan=0,totalPengeluaran=0,saldoAkhir=0; // FAST PATH: konfigurasi/frekuensi dan sheet periode tidak dibaca ulang // untuk setiap transaksi. }); if(detailRows.length)detailSh.getRange(detailSh.getLastRow()+1,1,detailRows.length,BKU_DETAIL_HEADERS_.length).setValues(detailRows.map(function(x){return BKU_DETAIL_HEADERS_.map(function(k){return x[k]===undefined?'':x[k];});})); const logSh=_ensureBkuImportLog_(); const now=Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),Session.getScriptTimeZone(),"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss"); // Agregasi sekali, lalu tulis log semua periode dalam satu setValues(). const periodStats={}; }); const logRows=Object.values(periodMap).map(function(p){ const s=periodStats[text(p.id_periode)]||{count:0,penerimaan:0,pengeluaran:0,saldo_akhir:0}; const v={id_import:importId,npsn:npsn,source_name:text(payload.source_name||'BKU.xlsx'),source_type:'EXCEL_BKU_ARKAS', tahun:p.tahun||'',id_periode:p.id_periode,nama_periode:p.nama_periode||'',total_source:sourceRows.length,total_valid:s.count, total_penerimaan:s.penerimaan,total_pengeluaran:s.pengeluaran,saldo_akhir:s.saldo_akhir,imported_at:now,status:'AKTIF'};
