const kodePengesahan='SPJ-'+Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyyMMdd')+'-'+Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g,'').substring(0,12).toUpperCase(); const baseUrl=ScriptApp.getService().getUrl() || ''; const payload={id_asesmen:text(idAsesmen),npsn:text(a.npsn),id_periode:text(a.id_periode),nilai_akhir:a.nilai_akhir,jenis:'PENGESAHAN_PENGAWAS',kode_pengesahan:kodePengesahan,verification_url:baseUrl+'?verify='+encodeURIComponent(kodePengesahan),ditandatangani_oleh:text(s.username||s.id),ditandatangani_at:new Date().toISOString(),integrity_hash:hash,signature_type:'INTERNAL_E_APPROVAL',versi_penilaian:new Date().getTime()}; sheet('pesan_koordinasi').appendRow([id,new Date(),text(s.username||s.id),text(a.npsn),'PENGESAHAN_PENILAIAN','PENGESAHAN PENILAIAN | '+idAsesmen,JSON.stringify(payload),'DISAHKAN']); return {success:true,message:'Penilaian berhasil disahkan secara elektronik di dalam aplikasi.',id_pesan:id,status:'DISAHKAN',integrity_hash:hash}; } function verifikasiPengesahanPenilaian(t,idAsesmen) { const s=validateSession(t); if(!s) throw new Error('Sesi tidak valid.'); const a=records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).find(function(x){return text(x.id_asesmen)===text(idAsesmen);}); if(!a) throw new Error('Asesmen tidak ditemukan.'); const x=_latestPengesahan_(idAsesmen); if(!x.signature) return {success:true,valid:false,message:'Belum ada pengesahan pengawas.'}; const current=_assessmentSnapshot_(a,idAsesmen), currentHash=_hashHex_(JSON.stringify(current)); return {success:true,valid:currentHash===text(x.signature.integrity_hash),stored_hash:text(x.signature.integrity_hash),current_hash:currentHash,data:x.signature}; } function verifikasiPengesahanByKode_(kode) { const k=text(kode).toUpperCase(); let sig=null; _pengesahanRows_('PENGESAHAN_PENILAIAN').some(function(m){ const p=_jsonPayload_(m); if(text(p.kode_pengesahan).toUpperCase()===k){ sig=Object.assign({},p,{id_pesan:m.id_pesan,tanggal:m.tanggal}); return true; } return false; }); if(!sig) return {success:true,valid:false,message:'Kode pengesahan tidak ditemukan.'}; const a=records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).find(function(x){return text(x.id_asesmen)===text(sig.id_asesmen);}); if(!a) return {success:true,valid:false,message:'Asesmen sumber tidak ditemukan.'}; const currentHash=_hashHex_(JSON.stringify(_assessmentSnapshot_(a,sig.id_asesmen))); const valid=currentHash===text(sig.integrity_hash); const school=_v5School_(a.npsn); return {success:true,valid:valid,message:valid?'Valid':'Hash penilaian berubah atau tidak cocok.',data:{kode_pengesahan:sig.kode_pengesahan,id_asesmen:sig.id_asesmen,npsn:a.npsn,nama_sekolah:school.nama_sekolah,id_periode:a.id_periode,nilai_akhir:a.nilai_akhir,ditandatangani_oleh:sig.ditandatangani_oleh,ditandatangani_at:sig.ditandatangani_at,integrity_hash:sig.integrity_hash}}; } /* Override orchestrator: cross-check lama -> risk severity -> simpan temuan. */ function jalankanCrossCheckSPJBulanan(t, p, n) { n = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, n); const calls = [ () => crossCheckTransaksiRKAS(t, p, n), () => crossCheckRealisasiRKAS(t, n, p), () => crossCheckTransaksiInvoice(t, p, n), () => crossCheckInvoiceBuktiPendukung(t, p, n), () => crossCheckKelengkapanDokumenSPJ(t, p, n), () => crossCheckPotensiDuplikatSPJ(t, p, n), () => crossCheckBuktiPendukung(t, p, n), () => crossCheckFotoDokumentasi(t, p, n), () => crossCheckTransaksiBukuPajak(t, p, n), () => crossCheckSSPPusat(t, p, n), () => crossCheckSSPDaerah(t, p, n), () => crossCheckPembayaranPajak(t, p, n), () => crossCheckTanggalBukti(t, p, n), () => crossCheckPihakPenerima(t, p, n), () => crossCheckDokumenRekonsiliasi(t, p, n) ]; let all = []; calls.forEach(f => { try { all = all.concat(hasilArray(f())); } catch(e) {} }); const session = getSession(t); const activeAssessment = session && upper(session.role) === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS ? records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).find(x => text(x.npsn) === n && text(x.id_periode) === text(p) && text(x.id_pengawas) === text(session.id) && upper(x.status_asesmen) !== 'SELESAI') : null; const preliminary = all .filter(x => x.id_transaksi || activeAssessment) .map(x => Object.assign({}, x, { npsn: n, id_periode: p, id_asesmen: x.id_asesmen || (activeAssessment ? activeAssessment.id_asesmen : '') })); const tr = canonicalBkuRows_(n,p); const rk = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).filter(x => text(x.npsn) === n && upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED'); const totalBudget = rk.reduce((s,x) => s + Math.max(0,num(x.pagu)),0); const codeFrequency = {}; preliminary.forEach(x => { const k = riskNorm_(x.kode_temuan); if(k) codeFrequency[k]=(codeFrequency[k]||0)+1; }); const context = { totalBudget: totalBudget, missingDocumentRatio: tr.length ? riskClamp_(preliminary.filter(x => /EVIDENCE|DOCUMENT|BUKTI/i.test([x.kode_temuan,x.sumber_utama].join(' '))).length / tr.length,0,1) : 0, codeFrequency: codeFrequency }; const enriched = preliminary.map(x => { const fs = riskFindingScore_(x, context); return Object.assign({}, x, { level_temuan: riskSeverity_(fs.score, x.level_temuan), hasil_sistem: x.hasil_sistem || 'PERLU_VERIFIKASI', catatan_sistem: (x.catatan_sistem || '') + ' Risiko algoritmik: ' + fs.score + '/100; tema: ' + fs.theme + '.', npsn: n, id_periode: p }); }); const keys = existingFindingKeys(), saved = []; enriched.forEach(x => { const r = simpanTemuanSPJUnik(x, keys); if (!r.duplicate) saved.push(x); }); return { success: true, npsn: n, id_periode: p, risk_engine: RISK_ENGINE.VERSION, jumlah_ditemukan: enriched.length, jumlah_baru: saved.length, data: saved }; } /* ============================================================================ * V20 — BKU AS SINGLE SOURCE OF TRANSACTIONS * RKAS = budget/reference; transaksi_spj = canonical BKU ledger; * Bank/Cash/Tax books are derived views from BKU, not independent sources. * This layer is additive and does NOT change the existing database schema. * ========================================================================== */ function v20BookFlags_(trx) { const u = upper(trx && trx.uraian_transaksi); const pay = upper(trx && trx.jenis_pembayaran); const proof = upper(trx && trx.nomor_bukti); const taxText = upper(trx && trx.pajak); const isTarik = /TARIK TUNAI|PENARIKAN TUNAI|AMBIL TUNAI/.test(u); const isPergeseranBank = /PERGESERAN UANG DI BANK/.test(u); const isBankNative = /TERIMA DANA|BUNGA BANK|PAJAK BUNGA|GIRO|REKENING|ATM|BIAYA ADMIN|ADMINISTRASI BANK/.test(u); const isBpu = /^BPU/.test(proof); const isBbu = /^BBU/.test(proof); // Pajak bunga adalah potongan bank atas bunga, bukan pasangan pungut-setor // pajak belanja sekolah. Tetap masuk Buku Bank, bukan Buku Pajak sekolah. const schoolTaxKeyword = !/PAJAK BUNGA/.test(u) && (/\bPPN\b|\bPPH\b|\bSSPD\b|SETOR PPN|SETOR PPH|TERIMA PPN|TERIMA PPH/.test(u + ' ' + taxText)); const bank = isPergeseranBank || isBankNative || isBbu || pay === 'BANK'; const cash = isTarik || isBpu || pay === 'TUNAI'; const tax = !!taxText || schoolTaxKeyword; const books = []; if (bank) books.push('BANK'); if (cash) books.push('KAS_TUNAI'); if (tax) books.push('PAJAK'); // Baris pajak tanpa bukti bisa tetap valid karena kanalnya akan diwarisi dari // transaksi belanja terdekat di _bkuDerivedBooks_. const needsReview = books.length === 0 && !tax; return { bank:bank, cash:cash, tax:tax, books:books, needs_review:needsReview }; } function v20RkasIndex_(npsn) { const rows = recordsByValue(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS, 'npsn', normalizeNpsn_(npsn)) .filter(x => upper(x.status_rkas) === 'APPROVED'); const byId = {}, byCode = {}; rows.forEach(r => { const rid = text(r.id_rkas), code = normalizeKodeBarang_(r.kode_rekening); if (rid) byId[rid] = r; if (code) { if (!byCode[code]) byCode[code] = []; byCode[code].push(r); } }); return {rows:rows, byId:byId, byCode:byCode}; } function v20ClassifyBkuTransaction_(trx, rkasIdx) { const flags = v20BookFlags_(trx); const rid = text(trx.id_rkas), code = normalizeKodeBarang_(trx.kode_rekening); let rkas = rid ? rkasIdx.byId[rid] : null; if (!rkas && code && rkasIdx.byCode[code] && rkasIdx.byCode[code].length === 1) rkas = rkasIdx.byCode[code][0]; const isBudget=_bkuIsBudgetRealization_(trx); const rkasStatus = isBudget ? (rkas ? 'COCOK' : (code ? 'RKAS_TIDAK_DITEMUKAN' : 'KODE_REKENING_KOSONG')) : 'NON_RKAS'; const reasons = []; if (isBudget && !rkas) reasons.push(rkasStatus); if (flags.needs_review) reasons.push('JENIS_PEMBAYARAN_BELUM_TERKLASIFIKASI'); if (flags.tax && !text(trx.pajak)) reasons.push('PAJAK_TERDETEKSI_DARI_URAIAN'); return Object.assign({}, trx, { v20_books: flags.books, v20_bank: flags.bank, v20_cash: flags.cash, v20_tax: flags.tax, v20_rkas_status: rkasStatus, v20_rkas_id: rkas ? text(rkas.id_rkas) : '', v20_rkas_pagu: rkas ? num(rkas.pagu) : 0, v20_needs_review: reasons.length > 0, v20_review_reasons: reasons }); } function _bkuDerivedBooks_(detailRows, trxRows, targetPeriod){ const txById={}; (trxRows||[]).forEach(function(x){txById[text(x.id_transaksi)]=x;}); const all=(detailRows||[]).map(function(d,i){ const tr=txById[text(d.id_transaksi)]||{}; return { source_row:num(d.source_row)||i+1, id_transaksi:text(d.id_transaksi), id_periode:text(d.id_periode), tanggal_transaksi:text(d.tanggal_transaksi), kode_kegiatan:text(d.kode_kegiatan), kode_rekening:text(d.kode_rekening), nomor_bukti:text(d.nomor_bukti), uraian:text(d.uraian), penerimaan:Math.max(0,num(d.penerimaan)), pengeluaran:Math.max(0,num(d.pengeluaran)), bku_saldo:num(d.saldo), jenis_belanja:text(tr.jenis_belanja), jenis_pembayaran:text(tr.jenis_pembayaran) }; }).sort(function(a,b){ const da=String(a.tanggal_transaksi||''), dbb=String(b.tanggal_transaksi||''); if(da!==dbb)return da.localeCompare(dbb); return a.source_row-b.source_row; }); function taxStem_(s){ return upper(s||'') .replace(/^(TERIMA|PUNGUT|SETOR|BAYAR)\s*/,'') .replace(/\s+/g,' ') .trim(); } function isNamedTax_(x){ return /\b(PPN|PPH|SSPD|PAJAK)\b/i.test(x.uraian||''); } function startsTaxFlow_(x){ return /^(TERIMA|PUNGUT|SETOR|BAYAR)\b/i.test(String(x.uraian||'').trim()); } function pairKey_(x){ return [x.tanggal_transaksi,x.kode_rekening,Math.max(x.penerimaan,x.pengeluaran)].join('|'); } // ARKAS tertentu menampilkan pajak hanya sebagai "Terima" dan "Setor". // Jika tanggal + kode rekening + nominal berpasangan, tetap dianggap arus pajak. const genericPair={}; all.forEach(function(x){ if(!startsTaxFlow_(x)||isNamedTax_(x))return; const k=pairKey_(x); if(!genericPair[k])genericPair[k]={in:0,out:0}; if(x.penerimaan>0)genericPair[k].in++; if(x.pengeluaran>0)genericPair[k].out++; }); const genericTaxKeys={}; Object.keys(genericPair).forEach(function(k){ if(genericPair[k].in>0&&genericPair[k].out>0)genericTaxKeys[k]=true; }); function isTax_(x){ return isNamedTax_(x) || !!genericTaxKeys[pairKey_(x)]; } function proofChannel_(proof){ const p=upper(proof||''); if(/^BBU/.test(p))return 'BANK'; if(/^BPU/.test(p))return 'TUNAI'; return ''; } // Untuk baris pajak tanpa nomor bukti, ikuti kanal transaksi belanja terdekat // dengan tanggal + kode rekening yang sama. Jika tidak ditemukan, default TUNAI. function inferTaxChannel_(idx,x){ for(let j=idx-1;j>=0&&j>=idx-12;j--){ const q=all[j]; if(q.tanggal_transaksi!==x.tanggal_transaksi)continue; if(x.kode_rekening&&q.kode_rekening!==x.kode_rekening)continue; if(isTax_(q))continue; const pc=proofChannel_(q.nomor_bukti); if(pc)return pc; if(upper(q.jenis_pembayaran).indexOf('BANK')>=0)return 'BANK'; } return 'TUNAI'; } function channel_(x,idx){ const u=upper(x.uraian||''), pc=proofChannel_(x.nomor_bukti); // Aturan sesuai alur BKU ARKAS pada file sekolah: // - Terima Dana / Pergeseran Uang di Bank / Bunga / Pajak Bunga -> Buku Bank // - Tarik Tunai -> Buku Kas Tunai // - BPU -> Buku Kas Tunai, BBU -> Buku Bank // - PPh/PPN/SSPD -> Buku Pajak dan mengikuti kanal transaksi asal. if(isTax_(x))return inferTaxChannel_(idx,x); if(/PERGESERAN UANG DI BANK/i.test(u))return 'BANK'; if(/TARIK TUNAI|PENARIKAN TUNAI|AMBIL TUNAI/i.test(u))return 'TUNAI'; if(/TERIMA DANA|BUNGA BANK|PAJAK BUNGA/i.test(u))return 'BANK'; if(pc)return pc; if(upper(x.jenis_pembayaran)==='BANK')return 'BANK'; if(upper(x.jenis_pembayaran)==='TUNAI')return 'TUNAI'; if(x.pengeluaran>0||x.penerimaan>0)return 'TUNAI'; return ''; } function bookFlow_(x,ch){ const u=upper(x.uraian||''); const amount=Math.max(0,num(x.penerimaan),num(x.pengeluaran)); // BKU mencatat pasangan transfer internal sebagai dua baris yang saling // menetralkan. Untuk buku pembantu arah ekonominya harus dibalik: // Pergeseran Uang di Bank = uang KELUAR dari bank. if(ch==='BANK' && /PERGESERAN UANG DI BANK/i.test(u)){ return {masuk:0,keluar:amount}; } // Tarik Tunai = uang MASUK ke kas tunai. if(ch==='TUNAI' && /TARIK TUNAI|PENARIKAN TUNAI|AMBIL TUNAI/i.test(u)){ return {masuk:amount,keluar:0}; } return {masuk:Math.max(0,num(x.penerimaan)),keluar:Math.max(0,num(x.pengeluaran))}; } let saldoBank=0,saldoTunai=0; const bank=[],cash=[],tax=[]; all.forEach(function(x,idx){ const ch=channel_(x,idx); const flow=bookFlow_(x,ch); if(ch==='BANK'){ saldoBank+=flow.masuk-flow.keluar; bank.push(Object.assign({},x,{masuk:flow.masuk,keluar:flow.keluar,saldo_buku:saldoBank,channel:'BANK'})); }else if(ch==='TUNAI'){ saldoTunai+=flow.masuk-flow.keluar; cash.push(Object.assign({},x,{masuk:flow.masuk,keluar:flow.keluar,saldo_buku:saldoTunai,channel:'TUNAI'})); } if(isTax_(x)){ const direction=x.penerimaan>0?'PUNGUT':'SETOR'; let jenis=taxStem_(x.uraian); if(!jenis)jenis='PAJAK (JENIS TIDAK TERBACA)'; tax.push(Object.assign({},x,{ arah:direction, jenis_pajak:jenis, nilai:x.penerimaan>0?x.penerimaan:x.pengeluaran, channel:ch })); } }); function currentRows_(arr){return arr.filter(function(x){return text(x.id_periode)===text(targetPeriod);});} const bankCurrent=currentRows_(bank),cashCurrent=currentRows_(cash),taxCurrent=currentRows_(tax); // Cross-check transfer bank -> kas tunai. // Pergeseran Uang di Bank = keluar Bank, Tarik Tunai = masuk Kas Tunai. const transferOut=bank.filter(function(x){return /PERGESERAN UANG DI BANK/i.test(x.uraian);}); const transferIn=cash.filter(function(x){return /TARIK TUNAI|PENARIKAN TUNAI|AMBIL TUNAI/i.test(x.uraian);}); const transferUsed={}; const transferIssues=[]; transferOut.forEach(function(o){ let found=-1; for(let i=0;i=0)transferUsed[found]=true; else transferIssues.push({kode:'PERGESERAN_BANK_TANPA_TARIK_TUNAI',tanggal:o.tanggal_transaksi,nilai:o.keluar,source_row:o.source_row}); }); transferIn.forEach(function(q,i){ if(!transferUsed[i])transferIssues.push({kode:'TARIK_TUNAI_TANPA_PERGESERAN_BANK',tanggal:q.tanggal_transaksi,nilai:q.masuk,source_row:q.source_row}); }); // Cross-check pajak pungut vs setor berdasarkan jenis + kode + nominal. const taxIn=tax.filter(function(x){return x.arah==='PUNGUT';}); const taxOut=tax.filter(function(x){return x.arah==='SETOR';}); const taxUsed={}; const taxIssues=[]; taxIn.forEach(function(pungut){ let found=-1; for(let i=0;i=0||setor.jenis_pajak.indexOf('TIDAK TERBACA')>=0; if(sameCode&&sameType&&Math.abs(pungut.nilai-setor.nilai)<0.5&&setor.tanggal_transaksi>=pungut.tanggal_transaksi){found=i;break;} } if(found>=0)taxUsed[found]=true; else taxIssues.push({kode:'PAJAK_PUNGUT_BELUM_DITEMUKAN_SETORAN',tanggal:pungut.tanggal_transaksi,jenis_pajak:pungut.jenis_pajak,nilai:pungut.nilai,source_row:pungut.source_row}); }); taxOut.forEach(function(setor,i){ if(!taxUsed[i])taxIssues.push({kode:'SETOR_PAJAK_TANPA_PUNGUTAN_PASANGAN',tanggal:setor.tanggal_transaksi,jenis_pajak:setor.jenis_pajak,nilai:setor.nilai,source_row:setor.source_row}); }); const currentAll=all.filter(function(x){return text(x.id_periode)===text(targetPeriod);}); const lastCurrent=currentAll.length?currentAll[currentAll.length-1]:null; const bkuClosing=lastCurrent?num(lastCurrent.bku_saldo):0; // Saldo bank/tunai saat akhir periode diperoleh dari row terakhir <= target. const periodIds={}; currentAll.forEach(function(x){periodIds[text(x.id_periode)]=true;}); const bankClosing=bankCurrent.length?num(bankCurrent[bankCurrent.length-1].saldo_buku): (bank.filter(function(x){return String(x.tanggal_transaksi)<=String(lastCurrent?.tanggal_transaksi||'');}).slice(-1)[0]?.saldo_buku||0); const cashClosing=cashCurrent.length?num(cashCurrent[cashCurrent.length-1].saldo_buku): (cash.filter(function(x){return String(x.tanggal_transaksi)<=String(lastCurrent?.tanggal_transaksi||'');}).slice(-1)[0]?.saldo_buku||0); const balanceDiff=(bankClosing+cashClosing)-bkuClosing; function summary_(arr){ return { count:arr.length, masuk:arr.reduce(function(s,x){return s+num(x.masuk);},0), keluar:arr.reduce(function(s,x){return s+num(x.keluar);},0), saldo_akhir:arr.length?num(arr[arr.length-1].saldo_buku):0 }; } const taxSummary={ count:taxCurrent.length, pungut:taxCurrent.filter(function(x){return x.arah==='PUNGUT';}).reduce(function(s,x){return s+num(x.nilai);},0), setor:taxCurrent.filter(function(x){return x.arah==='SETOR';}).reduce(function(s,x){return s+num(x.nilai);},0) }; taxSummary.saldo_pajak=taxSummary.pungut-taxSummary.setor; return { bank:{summary:summary_(bankCurrent),rows:bankCurrent}, cash:{summary:summary_(cashCurrent),rows:cashCurrent}, tax:{summary:taxSummary,rows:taxCurrent}, crosscheck:{ transfer:{ ok:transferIssues.length===0, issues:transferIssues, total_pergeseran_bank:transferOut.reduce(function(s,x){return s+x.keluar;},0), total_tarik_tunai:transferIn.reduce(function(s,x){return s+x.masuk;},0), // alias lama dipertahankan untuk kompatibilitas frontend lama total_pergeseran:transferOut.reduce(function(s,x){return s+x.keluar;},0), total_tarik:transferIn.reduce(function(s,x){return s+x.masuk;},0) }, tax:{ok:taxIssues.length===0,issues:taxIssues}, balance:{ok:Math.abs(balanceDiff)<0.5,bku_saldo_akhir:bkuClosing,bank_saldo_akhir:bankClosing,tunai_saldo_akhir:cashClosing,selisih:balanceDiff} } }; } function getBkuLedgerWorkbench(t, p, n) { requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const schoolNpsn = resolveSchoolNpsn(t, n); const period = text(p); if (!period) throw new Error('Periode wajib diisi.'); const trx = canonicalBkuRows_(schoolNpsn,period); const activeImport=_latestBkuImportForPeriod_(schoolNpsn,period); // Ambil seluruh baris pada snapshot yang sama agar saldo Buku Bank/Tunai dapat // dibawa maju dari periode sebelumnya; output tetap difilter ke periode dipilih. const sourceDetail=activeImport?_bkuDetailRecordsForImport_(activeImport.id_import):[]; const sourceByTx={};sourceDetail.forEach(function(x){if(text(x.id_transaksi))sourceByTx[text(x.id_transaksi)]=x;}); const totalBkuMovement = rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.max(0,num(x.nilai_transaksi)),0); const totalPajakPungut = rows.filter(x=>_bkuAccountingType_(x)==='PAJAK_PUNGUT').reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.max(0,num(x.nilai_transaksi)),0); const totalPajakSetor = rows.filter(x=>_bkuAccountingType_(x)==='PAJAK_SETOR').reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.max(0,num(x.nilai_transaksi)),0); const totalTransferInternal = rows.filter(x=>_bkuAccountingType_(x)==='TRANSFER_INTERNAL').reduce((s,x)=>s+Math.max(0,num(x.nilai_transaksi)),0); const books = { bank: rows.filter(x=>x.v20_bank), cash: rows.filter(x=>x.v20_cash), tax: rows.filter(x=>x.v20_tax), review: rows.filter(x=>x.v20_needs_review) }; const duplicateMap={}; rows.forEach(x=>{const k=upper(x.nomor_bukti); if(k) duplicateMap[k]=(duplicateMap[k]||0)+1;}); const duplicateIds=new Set(Object.keys(duplicateMap).filter(k=>duplicateMap[k]>1)); const exceptions=[]; rows.forEach(x=>{ (x.v20_review_reasons||[]).forEach(reason=>exceptions.push({ id_transaksi:x.id_transaksi, tanggal_transaksi:x.tanggal_transaksi, nomor_bukti:x.nomor_bukti, kode_rekening:x.kode_rekening, uraian_transaksi:x.uraian_transaksi, nilai_transaksi:x.nilai_transaksi, kode_exception:reason, level:reason==='RKAS_TIDAK_DITEMUKAN'?'MAJOR':'WARNING' })); if (duplicateIds.has(upper(x.nomor_bukti))) exceptions.push({ id_transaksi:x.id_transaksi, tanggal_transaksi:x.tanggal_transaksi, nomor_bukti:x.nomor_bukti, kode_rekening:x.kode_rekening, uraian_transaksi:x.uraian_transaksi, nilai_transaksi:x.nilai_transaksi, kode_exception:'NOMOR_BUKTI_DUPLIKAT', level:'MAJOR' }); }); // Validasi inti: hanya BELANJA_RKAS dibandingkan dengan pagu RKAS. const spendByRkas={}; budgetRows.forEach(function(x){ const key=text(x.v20_rkas_id||x.id_rkas||x.kode_rekening); if(!key)return; spendByRkas[key]=(spendByRkas[key]||0)+_bkuBudgetAmount_(x); }); rkasIdx.rows.forEach(function(r){ const key=text(r.id_rkas||r.kode_rekening),spent=num(spendByRkas[key]||0),pagu=Math.max(0,num(r.pagu)); if(spent>pagu+0.5){ exceptions.push({ id_transaksi:'',tanggal_transaksi:'',nomor_bukti:'',kode_rekening:text(r.kode_rekening), uraian_transaksi:text(r.uraian||r.nama_belanja_asli||'Realisasi rekening'), nilai_transaksi:spent,kode_exception:'REALISASI_RKAS_MELEBIHI_PAGU',level:'MAJOR', nilai_pagu:pagu,nilai_selisih:spent-pagu }); } }); if(totalPajakPungut>totalPajakSetor+0.5){ exceptions.push({ id_transaksi:'',tanggal_transaksi:'',nomor_bukti:'',kode_rekening:'', uraian_transaksi:'Pajak dipungut belum seluruhnya disetor', nilai_transaksi:totalPajakPungut-totalPajakSetor,kode_exception:'PAJAK_BELUM_DISETOR',level:'WARNING', nilai_pajak_pungut:totalPajakPungut,nilai_pajak_setor:totalPajakSetor }); }); (derivedBooks.crosscheck.tax.issues||[]).forEach(function(i){ exceptions.push({id_transaksi:'',tanggal_transaksi:i.tanggal||'',nomor_bukti:'',kode_rekening:'',uraian_transaksi:i.jenis_pajak||'Cross-check pajak',nilai_transaksi:num(i.nilai),kode_exception:i.kode,level:'WARNING',source_row:i.source_row||''}); }); if(!derivedBooks.crosscheck.balance.ok){ exceptions.push({id_transaksi:'',tanggal_transaksi:'',nomor_bukti:'',kode_rekening:'',uraian_transaksi:'Saldo Buku Bank + Buku Tunai tidak sama dengan saldo BKU',nilai_transaksi:Math.abs(num(derivedBooks.crosscheck.balance.selisih)),kode_exception:'SELISIH_SALDO_BUKU_DENGAN_BKU',level:'MAJOR'}); } const docs = recordsByValue(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ,'npsn',schoolNpsn) .filter(x=>text(x.id_periode)===period && upper(x.status)!=='NONAKTIF'); const masters=records(SHEETS.MASTER_SPJ); const mById={}; masters.forEach(x=>mById[text(x.id_spj)]=x); (latestDocBySpj[text(m.id_spj)]||null) : null; } return { success:true, version:'21.0', npsn:schoolNpsn, id_periode:period, architecture:'RKAS>EXCEL_BKU_CANONICAL>DERIVED_BOOKS>CROSSCHECK>EVIDENCE>ASSESSMENT', source: activeImport ? {id_import:text(activeImport.id_import),source_name:text(activeImport.source_name),source_type:text(activeImport.source_type),imported_at:text(activeImport.imported_at),total_valid:num(activeImport.total_valid),total_penerimaan:num(activeImport.total_penerimaan),total_pengeluaran:num(activeImport.total_pengeluaran),saldo_akhir:num(activeImport.saldo_akhir)} : {}, summary:{ total_rkas:totalRkas, // alias total_bku dipertahankan, tetapi maknanya sekarang REALISASI RKAS, // bukan jumlah seluruh arus kas BKU. total_bku:totalRealisasiRkas, total_realisasi_rkas:totalRealisasiRkas, total_bku_movement:totalBkuMovement, total_pajak_pungut:totalPajakPungut, total_pajak_setor:totalPajakSetor, total_transfer_internal:totalTransferInternal, sisa_anggaran:totalRkas-totalRealisasiRkas, total_transaksi:rows.length, total_transaksi_belanja:budgetRows.length, rkas_cocok:budgetRows.filter(x=>x.v20_rkas_status==='COCOK').length, perlu_review:books.review.length, jumlah_exception:exceptions.length }, books:{ bank:Object.assign({},derivedBooks.bank.summary,{document:docFor(/BUKU PEMBANTU BANK|BUKU BANK/)}), cash:Object.assign({},derivedBooks.cash.summary,{document:docFor(/KAS TUNAI/)}), tax:Object.assign({},derivedBooks.tax.summary,{document:docFor(/BUKU PEMBANTU PAJAK|BUKU PAJAK/)}) }, derived_books:derivedBooks, transactions:rows, exceptions:exceptions.slice(0,500), notes:[ 'Snapshot Excel BKU ARKAS terbaru adalah ledger transaksi utama untuk penilaian.', 'Realisasi RKAS hanya menghitung BELANJA_RKAS. } function simpanKoreksiKlasifikasiBku(t, d) { d=d||{}; const s=requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]); const n=normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn||s.username); const idTrx=text(d.id_transaksi); if(!idTrx) throw new Error('ID transaksi wajib diisi.'); if(idc<1||nc<1||pc<1||taxc<1||sc<1) throw new Error('Schema transaksi belum lengkap.'); const last=sh.getLastRow(); if(last<2) throw new Error('Transaksi tidak ditemukan.'); const vals=sh.getRange(2,1,last-1,h.length).getValues(); for(let i=0;i=0 && s.indexOf('.')<0){ if(/^-?\d{1,3}(,\d{3})+$/.test(s)) s=s.replace(/,/g,''); else s=s.replace(',','.'); }else if(s.indexOf('.')>=0 && !/^-?\d+\.\d{1,2}$/.test(s)) s=s.replace(/\./g,''); const n=Number(s); return Number.isFinite(n)?Math.round(n):null; } function _pdfBookAmountsFromLine_(line){ const tokens=String(line||'').match(/(?:Rp\.?\s*)?-?\d[\d\s.,]{2,}/gi)||[]; return tokens.map(_pdfBookParseAmount_).filter(function(x){return x!==null&&Math.abs(x)>=1;}); } function _pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,labels,preferBottom){ const lines=String(ocrText||'').split(/\r?\n/).map(function(x){return x.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}).filter(Boolean); const aliases=(labels||[]).map(upper), order=[]; if(preferBottom){for(let i=lines.length-1;i>=0;i--)order.push(i);} else{for(let i=0;i=0;}))continue; for(let j=i;j<=Math.min(lines.length-1,i+2);j++){ const vals=_pdfBookAmountsFromLine_(lines[j]); if(vals.length)return vals[vals.length-1]; } } return null; } function _extractDerivedBookPdfTotals_(ocrText,bookType){ const t=upper(bookType); if(t==='PAJAK'){ return { pungut:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['TOTAL PUNGUTAN','JUMLAH PUNGUTAN','PAJAK DIPUNGUT','TOTAL PENERIMAAN PAJAK'],true), setor:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['TOTAL SETORAN','JUMLAH SETORAN','PAJAK DISETOR','TOTAL PENGELUARAN PAJAK'],true), saldo_pajak:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['SALDO PAJAK','SALDO AKHIR'],true) }; } return { masuk:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['TOTAL PENERIMAAN','JUMLAH PENERIMAAN','TOTAL MASUK','JUMLAH MASUK'],true), keluar:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['TOTAL PENGELUARAN','JUMLAH PENGELUARAN','TOTAL KELUAR','JUMLAH KELUAR'],true), saldo_akhir:_pdfBookFindAmount_(ocrText,['SALDO AKHIR','SALDO BUKU','SALDO'],true) }; } function _compareDerivedPdfFields_(expected,actual,fields){ const details=[];let mismatch=false,unread=false; (fields||[]).forEach(function(f){ const e=Math.round(num(expected&&expected[f.key])); const a=actual&&actual[f.key]!==null&&actual[f.key]!==undefined?Math.round(num(actual[f.key])):null; const diff=a===null?null:a-e; const readable=a!==null,match=readable&&Math.abs(diff)<=1; if(!readable)unread=true; else if(!match)mismatch=true; details.push({key:f.key,label:f.label,expected:e,pdf:a,difference:diff,readable:readable,match:match}); }); return {status:mismatch?'SELISIH':(unread?'PERLU_VERIFIKASI':'COCOK'),fields:details,all_readable:!unread,all_match:!mismatch&&!unread}; } function crossCheckDerivedBookPdf(t,d){ d=d||{}; requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH,CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS]); if(type==='PAJAK'&&num(expected.count)===0){ return {success:true,status:'TIDAK_BERLAKU',book_type:type,npsn:n,id_periode:p,message:'Tidak ada transaksi pajak pada Excel BKU periode ini.',expected:expected,pdf:null,comparison:null}; } if(!doc||!text(doc.file_id)){ return {success:true,status:'PDF_BELUM_UPLOAD',book_type:type,npsn:n,id_periode:p,message:'PDF buku pembanding belum diunggah.',expected:expected,pdf:null,comparison:null}; } const fileId=text(d.fileId||d.file_id||doc.file_id); if(fileId!==text(doc.file_id))throw new Error('File PDF tidak sesuai dengan dokumen aktif buku ini.'); const cache=CacheService.getScriptCache(),cacheKey='V217PDFCMP|'+fileId+'|'+p+'|'+type; [{key:'pungut',label:'Pajak dipungut'},{key:'setor',label:'Pajak disetor'},{key:'saldo_pajak',label:'Saldo pajak'}] : [{key:'masuk',label:'Penerimaan'},{key:'keluar',label:'Pengeluaran'},{key:'saldo_akhir',label:'Saldo akhir'}]; const comparison=_compareDerivedPdfFields_(expected,actual,fields); const result={ success:true,status:comparison.status,book_type:type,npsn:n,id_periode:p, document:{id_dokumen:text(doc.id_dokumen),file_id:text(doc.file_id),nama_file:text(doc.nama_file),versi:num(doc.versi)}, expected:expected,pdf:actual,comparison:comparison,ocr_characters:num(ocr.characters), message:comparison.status==='COCOK'?'Nilai hasil distribusi Excel BKU cocok dengan PDF.': (comparison.status==='SELISIH'?'Ditemukan perbedaan nilai antara Excel BKU dan PDF.':'Sebagian total pada PDF belum dapat dibaca otomatis. Lakukan verifikasi manual.') }; try{cache.put(cacheKey,JSON.stringify(result),21600);}catch(_){} return result; } function ocrBkuReconciliation(t, fileId, p, n) { const ocr=ocrDokumenPdf(t,fileId,p,n); const wb=getBkuLedgerWorkbench(t,p,n); const txt=upper(ocr.text||''); const matched=[]; (wb.transactions||[]).forEach(tr=>{ const proof=upper(tr.nomor_bukti), code=upper(tr.kode_rekening), amount=Math.round(num(tr.nilai_transaksi)); if(amount>0 && (txt.indexOf(amountPlain)>=0 || txt.indexOf(amountDot)>=0)) hits.push('NILAI'); if(hits.length) matched.push({id_transaksi:tr.id_transaksi,nomor_bukti:tr.nomor_bukti,kode_rekening:tr.kode_rekening,nilai_transaksi:tr.nilai_transaksi,hits:hits}); }); return {success:true,source:'OCR_BKU_RECONCILIATION',file_id:fileId,characters:ocr.characters||0, total_transaksi:wb.summary.total_transaksi,total_terindikasi_cocok:matched.length, match_ratio:wb.summary.total_transaksi?Math.round(matched.length/wb.summary.total_transaksi*10000)/100:0, matches:matched.slice(0,500),text:ocr.text||''}; } /* Dispatcher lama dipindahkan secara tekstual oleh patch ini agar action tetap sama. */ function ubahPasswordSendiri(t, passwordLama, passwordBaru, konfirmasiPassword) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS]); const oldPw = String(passwordLama || ''); const newPw = String(passwordBaru || ''); const confirm = String(konfirmasiPassword || ''); if (!oldPw || !newPw || !confirm) throw new Error('Password lama, password baru, dan konfirmasi wajib diisi.'); if (newPw !== confirm) throw new Error('Konfirmasi password baru tidak sama.'); if (newPw.length < 6) throw new Error('Password baru minimal 6 karakter.'); if (oldPw === newPw) throw new Error('Password baru harus berbeda dari password lama.'); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.USERS), h = headers(SHEETS.USERS); const idCol = h.indexOf('id') + 1, userCol = h.indexOf('username') + 1, passCol = h.indexOf('password') + 1, statusCol = h.indexOf('status') + 1; if (idCol < 1 || passCol < 1) throw new Error('Struktur tabel users tidak lengkap.'); for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { const uid = text(sh.getRange(r, idCol).getValue()); const uname = userCol > 0 ? text(sh.getRange(r, userCol).getValue()) : ''; if (uid === text(s.id) || uname === text(s.username)) { if (statusCol > 0 && upper(sh.getRange(r, statusCol).getValue()) !== 'AKTIF') throw new Error('Akun tidak aktif.'); const hh = h.indexOf('password_hash') + 1, ss = h.indexOf('password_salt') + 1; const storedHash = hh > 0 ? text(sh.getRange(r, hh).getValue()) : ''; const storedSalt = ss > 0 ? text(sh.getRange(r, ss).getValue()) : ''; const legacy = passCol > 0 ? String(sh.getRange(r, passCol).getValue() || '') : ''; const validOld = storedHash && storedSalt ? verifyPassword_(oldPw, storedHash, storedSalt) : legacy === oldPw; if (!validOld) throw new Error('Password lama tidak benar.'); writeUserPassword_(sh, r, newPw); return { success:true, message:'Password berhasil diubah. Password baru berlaku pada login berikutnya.' }; } } throw new Error('Data akun tidak ditemukan.'); } function simpanBuktiTemuanSPJ(t, data) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS]); data = data || {}; const idAsesmen = text(data.id_asesmen), idTemuan = text(data.id_temuan), idIndikator = text(data.id_indikator); const page = text(data.halaman_bukti); if (!idAsesmen || !idTemuan || !idIndikator || !page) throw new Error('Asesmen, temuan, indikator, dan halaman bukti wajib diisi.'); if (!/^\d+$/.test(page) || Number(page) < 1) throw new Error('Nomor halaman PDF tidak valid.'); const asesmen = records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).find(a => text(a.id_asesmen) === idAsesmen && text(a.id_pengawas) === text(s.id)); if (!asesmen) throw new Error('Asesmen tidak ditemukan atau bukan tugas Anda.'); if (upper(asesmen.status_asesmen) === 'SELESAI') throw new Error('Asesmen sudah difinalisasi dan bukti tidak dapat diubah.'); const finding = records(SHEETS.TEMUAN_SPJ).find(x => text(x.id_temuan) === idTemuan); if (!finding) throw new Error('Temuan tidak ditemukan.'); let findingValid = text(finding.id_asesmen) === idAsesmen; if (!findingValid && text(finding.id_transaksi)) { const trx = records(SHEETS.TRANSAKSI_SPJ).find(x => text(x.id_transaksi) === text(finding.id_transaksi)); findingValid = !!trx && text(trx.npsn) === text(asesmen.npsn) && text(trx.id_periode) === text(asesmen.id_periode); } if (!findingValid) throw new Error('Temuan tidak berasal dari sekolah/periode asesmen ini.'); const indicators = hitungIndikatorDariTemuan(t, asesmen.id_periode, asesmen.npsn, idAsesmen).data || []; if (!indicators.some(x => text(x.id_indikator) === idIndikator)) throw new Error('Indikator tidak tersedia pada asesmen ini.'); const sh = sheet(SHEETS.DETAIL_ASESMEN), h = headers(SHEETS.DETAIL_ASESMEN); const ac = h.indexOf('id_asesmen') + 1, ic = h.indexOf('id_indikator') + 1; const now = new Date(); for (let r = 2; r <= sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r, ac).getValue()) === idAsesmen && text(sh.getRange(r, ic).getValue()) === idIndikator) { const payload = {id_temuan:idTemuan, halaman_bukti:page, updated_at:now}; h.forEach(k => { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(payload,k)) sh.getRange(r,h.indexOf(k)+1).setValue(payload[k]); }); return {success:true,updated:true,id_asesmen:idAsesmen,id_temuan:idTemuan,id_indikator:idIndikator,halaman_bukti:Number(page)}; } } const auto = indicators.find(x => text(x.id_indikator) === idIndikator); return insert(SHEETS.DETAIL_ASESMEN, { id_detail:id('DET'), id_asesmen:idAsesmen, id_indikator:idIndikator, status_berlaku:'BERLAKU', skor:'', alasan_na:'', hasil_otomatis:num(auto && auto.skor_otomatis), hasil_verifikasi:'OTOMATIS', id_temuan:idTemuan, catatan_pengawas:'', halaman_bukti:page, updated_at:now }); } function getPdfEvidenceFeed(t, idPeriode, npsn, idAsesmenOpt) { const idP = text(idPeriode); if (!idP) throw new Error('Periode wajib diisi.'); const auth = _v5AuthorizedSchool_(t, npsn); const n = auth.npsn; const session = auth.session; const requestedId = text(idAsesmenOpt); // Bila Pengawas sedang memeriksa asesmennya sendiri, feed harus dikunci pada // id_asesmen tersebut. Versi lama memilih asesmen lain yang sudah SELESAI, // sehingga halaman bukti/skor dapat berasal dari penilaian Pengawas berbeda. let asesmen = null; if (requestedId) { asesmen = records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).find(function(a){ return text(a.id_asesmen) === requestedId && normalizeNpsn_(a.npsn) === n && text(a.id_periode) === idP; }) || null; if (!asesmen) throw new Error('Asesmen yang diminta tidak ditemukan pada sekolah/periode ini.'); if (upper(session.role) === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS && text(asesmen.id_pengawas) !== text(session.id)) { throw new Error('Asesmen tersebut bukan milik Pengawas yang sedang login.'); } } else { const rows = records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ).filter(function(a){ return normalizeNpsn_(a.npsn) === n && text(a.id_periode) === idP; }); rows.sort(function(a,b){ const sa = upper(a.status_asesmen)==='SELESAI' ? 1 : 0; const sb = upper(b.status_asesmen)==='SELESAI' ? 1 : 0; if (sa !== sb) return sb-sa; return String(b.tanggal_selesai || b.tanggal_mulai || '').localeCompare(String(a.tanggal_selesai || a.tanggal_mulai || '')); }); asesmen = rows[0] || null; } if (!asesmen) throw new Error('Penilaian untuk sekolah dan periode tersebut belum tersedia.'); const details = records(SHEETS.DETAIL_ASESMEN).filter(function(x){ return text(x.id_asesmen) === text(asesmen.id_asesmen); }); const byPage = {}; details.forEach(function(d){ const p = text(d.halaman_bukti); if (!p) return; if (!byPage[p]) byPage[p] = []; byPage[p].push(d); }); return { success:true, data:{ paket_version:'V10.1', id_asesmen:text(asesmen.id_asesmen), id_periode:idP, npsn:n, asesmen:asesmen, detail_asesmen:details, bukti_per_halaman:byPage } }; } /* ============================================================================ * SIMPEL SPJ V5 — TUTORIAL FEED + PAKET ARSIP PENILAIAN * Basis: alur V4 yang diberikan pengguna. * Prinsip: tidak menambah sheet/kolom/tabel. Memakai data yang sudah ada. * ========================================================================== */ function _v5AuthorizedSchool_(t, npsn) { const s = validateSession(t), role = upper(s.role), n = normalizeNpsn_(npsn); if (![CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS].includes(role)) { throw new Error('Anda tidak memiliki hak akses.'); } if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH) { const own = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (own !== n) throw new Error('Data bukan milik sekolah Anda.'); } else if (role === CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS) { resolveSchoolNpsn(t, n); } return {session:s,npsn:n}; } /* Tutorial resmi aplikasi berada di GitHub. Tidak perlu tabel baru dan tidak lagi membaca file tutorial dari Google Drive. */ function getTutorialSimpelSpj(t) { const s = validateSession(t); if (!s) throw new Error('Sesi tidak valid.'); const url = 'https://github.com/DikporaRembang/simpelspj/blob/main/tutorial_simpelspj.pdf'; const rawUrl = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/DikporaRembang/simpelspj/main/tutorial_simpelspj.pdf'; return { success:true, available:true, data:{ name:'tutorial_simpelspj.pdf', file_id:'', file_url:url, preview_url:rawUrl, download_url:rawUrl, inline_data_url:'', source:'GITHUB' } }; } function _v5Rows_(sheetName, predicate) { const rows = records(sheetName); return predicate ? rows.filter(predicate) : rows; } function _v5School_(npsn) { const map = getSekolahMap(); return map[normalizeNpsn_(npsn)] || {npsn:normalizeNpsn_(npsn),nama_sekolah:'',kecamatan:'',alamat_lengkap:''}; } function _v5LatestAppeals_(idAsesmen) { if (typeof _appealMessageRows_ !== 'function' || typeof _appealPayload_ !== 'function') return []; return _appealMessageRows_() .map(function(m){ return Object.assign({},_appealPayload_(m),{_row:m._row,id_pesan:m.id_pesan,status_keberatan:m.status_keberatan||'DIAJUKAN'}); }) .filter(function(x){ return text(x.id_asesmen)===text(idAsesmen); }) .sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.diajukan_at||a.tanggal||'').localeCompare(String(b.diajukan_at||b.tanggal||'')); }); } function getPaketPenilaianSPJ(t, idPeriode, npsn) { const idP = text(idPeriode); }); asesmenRows.sort(function(a,b){ const sa = upper(a.status_asesmen)==='SELESAI' ? 1 : 0; const sb = upper(b.status_asesmen)==='SELESAI' ? 1 : 0; if (sa !== sb) return sb-sa; return String(b.tanggal_selesai || b.tanggal_mulai || '').localeCompare(String(a.tanggal_selesai || a.tanggal_mulai || '')); }); const asesmen = asesmenRows[0] || null; if (!asesmen) { throw new Error('Penilaian untuk sekolah dan periode tersebut belum tersedia.'); } /* Seluruh bahan dikunci pada sekolah + periode, bukan data global. */ const rkas = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).filter(function(x){ return normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===n && (!text(x.status_rkas) || upper(x.status_rkas)==='APPROVED'); }); const transaksi = records(SHEETS.TRANSAKSI_SPJ).filter(function(x){ return normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===n && text(x.id_periode)===idP; }); const dokumen = records(SHEETS.DOKUMEN_SPJ).filter(function(x){ return normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===n && text(x.id_periode)===idP && upper(x.status)!=='NONAKTIF'; }).sort(function(a,b){return num(b.versi)-num(a.versi);}); const trxIds = new Set(transaksi.map(function(x){ return text(x.id_transaksi); }).filter(Boolean)); const temuan = records(SHEETS.TEMUAN_SPJ).filter(function(x){ const linkedAssessment = text(x.id_asesmen) === text(asesmen.id_asesmen);
