}); const appeals = _v5LatestAppeals_(asesmen.id_asesmen); const latest = typeof _latestPengesahan_ === 'function' ? _latestPengesahan_(asesmen.id_asesmen) : {approval:null,signature:null}; return { success:true, paket_version:'V10', id_asesmen:text(asesmen.id_asesmen), id_periode:idP, npsn:n, sekolah:_v5School_(n), asesmen:asesmen, rkas:rkas, transaksi:transaksi, dokumen:dokumen, temuan:temuan, detail_asesmen:detail, riwayat_keberatan:appeals, persetujuan_sekolah:latest.approval, pengesahan_pengawas:latest.signature, ringkasan:{ jumlah_rkas:rkas.length, jumlah_transaksi:transaksi.length, jumlah_dokumen:dokumen.length, jumlah_temuan:temuan.length, jumlah_detail_asesmen:detail.length, jumlah_keberatan:appeals.length } }; } /* Endpoint ringan untuk daftar arsip yang tersedia tanpa memuat semua data. */ function getDaftarArsipPenilaianV5(t) { const s=validateSession(t), role=upper(s.role); if (![CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH,CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS,CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS].includes(role)) throw new Error('Anda tidak memiliki hak akses.'); let rows=records(SHEETS.ASESMEN_SPJ); if(role===CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH){ const n=normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn||s.username); rows=rows.filter(x=>normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)===n); } else if(role===CONFIG.ROLES.PENGAWAS){ rows=rows.filter(x=>{try{resolveSchoolNpsn(t,x.npsn);return true;}catch(e){return false;}}); } return {success:true,data:rows.map(function(a){ const p=typeof _latestPengesahan_==='function'?_latestPengesahan_(a.id_asesmen):{approval:null,signature:null}; return {id_asesmen:text(a.id_asesmen),npsn:normalizeNpsn_(a.npsn),id_periode:text(a.id_periode), status_asesmen:text(a.status_asesmen),nilai_akhir:a.nilai_akhir, disetujui_sekolah:!!p.approval,disahkan_pengawas:!!p.signature}; })}; } /* ============================================================================ * RKAS IMPORT & MAPPING ENGINE — INTEGRATED V1 * ========================================================================== */ /* ============================================================================ * SIMPEL SPJ — RKAS IMPORT & MAPPING ENGINE V1 * Target: SIMPEL_SPJ_V14_1_STATUS_SEKOLAH_BACKEND.gs * * CARA PASANG: * 1. Tambahkan/ubah EXPECTED_HEADERS.master_rkas sesuai struktur database terbaru. * 2. Tambahkan EXPECTED_HEADERS.rkas_import. * 3. Tambahkan fungsi-fungsi pada file ini ke backend utama. * 4. Tambahkan 3 action pada doPost() sesuai petunjuk di bagian paling bawah. * * CATATAN: * - Modul ini menangani NORMALISASI, MAPPING, PREVIEW dan COMMIT. * - Parser XLSX/PDF belum termasuk di sini. Parser harus menghasilkan * payload.rows dengan format internal yang dijelaskan pada importRKASPreview(). * - Mapping kegiatan menggunakan data sheet maping_kegiatan yang saat ini * diketahui memiliki header: Kode Kegiatan, Program, Sub Program, Uraian. * - Karena maping_kegiatan saat ini tidak memiliki kolom id pada sumber yang * diperiksa, id_kegiatan dibuat dari kode kegiatan bila tersedia, atau * fingerprint teks. Jangan mengarang ID yang tidak ada di master. * ========================================================================== */ /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 1. STRUKTUR HEADER * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ // Ganti entry master_rkas di EXPECTED_HEADERS dengan struktur terbaru Anda: // // master_rkas: [ // 'id_rkas', // 'npsn', // 'id_import', // 'kode_rekening', // 'id_referensi_barang', // 'nama_belanja_asli', // 'uraian', // 'volume', // 'satuan', // 'harga_satuan', // 'pagu', // 'status_rkas', // 'tanggal_pengesahan', // 'sumber_data', // 'file_rkas_id', // 'nomor_baris_sumber', // 'status_mapping', // 'catatan_mapping', // 'update_at' // ], // // Tambahkan: // // rkas_import: [ // 'id_import', // 'npsn', // 'nama_file', // 'jenis_file', // 'tahun', // 'versi_rkas', // 'tanggal_upload', // 'uploaded_by', // 'file_id', // 'status_import', // 'total_baris', // 'berhasil_mapping', // 'perlu_konfirmasi', // 'gagal_mapping', // 'created_at' // ], /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 2. KONSTANTA TAMBAHAN * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ const RKAS_MAP_STATUS = { MATCHED: 'MATCHED', REVIEW: 'REVIEW', UNMATCHED: 'UNMATCHED', MANUAL: 'MANUAL' }; /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 3. NORMALISASI * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function normalizeRkasText_(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v) .replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ') .normalize('NFKC') .toLowerCase() .replace(/[“”"']/g, '') .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s./()&-]/gi, ' ') .replace(/\s+/g, ' ') .trim(); } function normalizeRkasCode_(v) { let s = String(v == null ? '' : v) .replace(/\u00A0/g, '') .replace(/^'+/, '') .trim() .replace(/\s+/g, ''); // Google Sheets/Excel kadang mengirim kode sebagai teks numerik dengan .0. if (/^\d+(?:\.0+)$/.test(s)) s = s.replace(/\.0+$/, ''); return s; } // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // DETEKSI SUBTOTAL RKAS // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Baris subtotal sering mempunyai kode rekening yang sama dengan item di // bawahnya, tetapi uraian parent tidak diawali nomor item. Contoh: // 5.1.02.... | Belanja Alat/Bahan ... | 805500 // 5.1.02.... | 001. Alat Olah Raga Bola ... | 805500 // Parent tidak boleh menjadi item master_rkas. Hanya item detail yang diimpor. function _rkasIsNumberedItem_(v) { return /^\s*\d{1,4}\s*[.)\-:]\s+/.test(String(v || '')); } function _rkasSubtotalDescription_(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v) .replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ') .normalize('NFKC') .toLowerCase() .replace(/^[\s\-:]*\d{1,4}\s*[.)\-:]\s*/, '') .replace(/[\-–—]+$/, '') .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s./()&-]/gi, ' ') .replace(/\s+/g, ' ') .trim(); } function _rkasIsSubtotalRow_(rows, i) { const cur = rows[i]; if (!cur) return false; const code = normalizeRkasCode_(cur.kode_rekening); const amount = Number(cur.pagu || 0); const desc = text(cur.nama_belanja_asli || cur.nama_belanja || cur.uraian || ''); if (!code || !(amount > 0) || _rkasIsNumberedItem_(desc)) return false; const children = []; let sum = 0; for (let j = i + 1; j < rows.length; j++) { const r = rows[j] || {}; const childCode = normalizeRkasCode_(r.kode_rekening); if (!childCode) continue; if (childCode !== code) break; const childDesc = text(r.nama_belanja_asli || r.nama_belanja || r.uraian || ''); if (!_rkasIsNumberedItem_(childDesc)) break; const p = Number(r.pagu || 0); if (!(p > 0)) break; children.push(r); sum += p; } if (!children.length) return false; const tolerance = Math.max(0.01, Math.abs(amount) * 0.000001); const labeled = /\b(subtotal|jumlah|total(?:\s+belanja)?)\b/i.test(desc); return Math.abs(sum - amount) <= tolerance || (labeled && children.length > 0); } function removeRkasSubtotalRows_(rows) { const arr = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []; return arr.filter((r, i) => !_rkasIsSubtotalRow_(arr, i)); } function normalizeRkasNumber_(v) { if (v === '' || v == null) return 0; return num(v); } function tokenizeRkas_(v) { const s = normalizeRkasText_(v); if (!s) return []; return s.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); } function jaccardRkas_(a, b) { const A = new Set(tokenizeRkas_(a)); const B = new Set(tokenizeRkas_(b)); if (!A.size || !B.size) return 0; let intersection = 0; A.forEach(x => { if (B.has(x)) intersection++; }); const union = new Set([...A, ...B]).size; return union ? intersection / union : 0; } function similarityRkasText_(a, b) { const A = normalizeRkasText_(a); const B = normalizeRkasText_(b); if (!A || !B) return 0; if (A === B) return 1; const shorter = A.length <= B.length ? A : B; const longer = A.length <= B.length ? B : A; // containment diberi nilai tinggi, tetapi tidak otomatis final. if (longer.indexOf(shorter) !== -1) { return Math.min(0.96, shorter.length / longer.length + 0.20); } return jaccardRkas_(A, B); } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 4. MEMBACA MASTER REFERENSI * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function getRkasReferensiBarangRows_() { const index = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); return Object.keys(index.byCode || {}).map(k => { const x = index.byCode[k]; return { kode_barang: x.kode_barang, rincian_obyek: x.rincian_obyek }; }); } function getRkasMapingKegiatanRows_() { const cacheKey = 'simpel_rkas_maping_kegiatan_v1'; const cached = getCachedJson_(cacheKey); if (cached) return cached; const rows = records(SHEETS.MAPING_KEGIATAN); const result = rows.map(x => ({ kode_kegiatan: normalizeRkasCode_(x['Kode Kegiatan'] || x.kode_kegiatan), program: text(x.Program || x.program), sub_program: text(x['Sub Program'] || x.sub_program), uraian: text(x.Uraian || x.uraian) })).filter(x => x.kode_kegiatan || x.program || x.sub_program || x.uraian ); putCachedJson_(cacheKey, result, 600); return result; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 5. MAPPING KEGIATAN * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function _rkasBuildFastMappingContext_() { const ref = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const kegiatanRows = getRkasMapingKegiatanRows_(); const kegiatanByCode = {}; const kegiatanByName = {}; kegiatanRows.forEach(x => { const code = normalizeRkasCode_(x.kode_kegiatan); if (code) { if (!kegiatanByCode[code]) kegiatanByCode[code] = []; kegiatanByCode[code].push(x); } const label = normalizeRkasText_([x.uraian, x.sub_program, x.program].filter(Boolean).join(' ')); if (label) { if (!kegiatanByName[label]) kegiatanByName[label] = []; kegiatanByName[label].push(x); } }); return { refIndex: ref, kegiatanRows, kegiatanByCode, kegiatanByName }; } function mapRkasKegiatan_(row, fastCtx) { const ctx = fastCtx || _rkasBuildFastMappingContext_(); const rows = ctx.kegiatanRows || []; const byCode = ctx.kegiatanByCode || {}; const sourceCode = normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_kegiatan || row.kode_program || ''); const sourceName = text(row.kegiatan || row.nama_kegiatan || row.uraian_kegiatan || ''); if (sourceCode && byCode[sourceCode]) { const exact = byCode[sourceCode]; const ref = exact[0]; return { status: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score: 100, id_kegiatan: ref.kode_kegiatan || '', kode_kegiatan: ref.kode_kegiatan || sourceCode, kegiatan: ref.uraian || ref.sub_program || ref.program || sourceName, kandidat: exact }; } if (!sourceName) return { status: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED, score: 0, id_kegiatan: '', kode_kegiatan: sourceCode, kegiatan: '', kandidat: [] }; const candidates = rows.map(x => { const label = [x.uraian, x.sub_program, x.program].filter(Boolean).join(' '); return Object.assign({}, x, {_score: similarityRkasText_(sourceName, label)}); }).sort((a,b)=>b._score-a._score).slice(0,5); const best = candidates[0]; if (!best || best._score < 0.68) return { status: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED, score: best ? Math.round(best._score*100):0, id_kegiatan:'', kode_kegiatan:sourceCode, kegiatan:sourceName, kandidat:candidates }; // Otomatis menerima kecocokan yang cukup kuat. Ambiguitas kecil tidak lagi // menghentikan proses impor; kandidat tetap dikirim sebagai informasi. const confidence = best._score >= 0.80 ? 100 : Math.round(best._score*100); return { status: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score: confidence, id_kegiatan: best.kode_kegiatan || '', kode_kegiatan: best.kode_kegiatan || sourceCode, kegiatan: best.uraian || best.sub_program || best.program || sourceName, kandidat: candidates, match_type: best._score >= 0.88 ? 'AUTO_NAME_HIGH' : 'AUTO_NAME' }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 6. MAPPING BARANG * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function mapRkasBarang_(row, kegiatanResult, fastCtx) { const ctx = fastCtx || _rkasBuildFastMappingContext_(); const idx = ctx.refIndex || {byCode:{}, byName:{}}; const byCode = idx.byCode || {}; const byName = idx.byName || {}; const sourceCode = normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_rekening || row.kode_barang || ''); const sourceName = text(row.nama_belanja || row.nama_belanja_asli || row.uraian || ''); if (sourceCode && byCode[sourceCode]) { const ref = byCode[sourceCode]; const score = sourceName && ref.rincian_obyek ? Math.round((0.75 + 0.25 * similarityRkasText_(sourceName, ref.rincian_obyek)) * 100) : 100; return {status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score:Math.max(score,95), id_referensi_barang:ref.kode_barang, kode_barang:ref.kode_barang, nama_referensi:ref.rincian_obyek, kandidat:[ref], match_type:'EXACT_CODE'}; } if (sourceName) { const nameKey = normalizeRkasText_(sourceName); if (nameKey && byName[nameKey]) { const ref = byName[nameKey]; return {status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score:99, id_referensi_barang:ref.kode_barang, kode_barang:ref.kode_barang, nama_referensi:ref.rincian_obyek, kandidat:[ref], match_type:'EXACT_NAME'}; } } if (!sourceName) return {status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED,score:0, id_referensi_barang:'',kode_barang:sourceCode,nama_referensi:'',kandidat:[]}; // Cari hanya kandidat terbaik dari master yang sudah di-index. Tidak ada // filter Spreadsheet per baris. const candidates = Object.keys(byName).map(k => { const ref = byName[k]; return Object.assign({}, ref, {_score: similarityRkasText_(sourceName, ref.rincian_obyek)}); }).sort((a,b)=>b._score-a._score).slice(0,5); const best = candidates[0]; if (!best || best._score < 0.68) return { status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED, score:best?Math.round(best._score*100):0, id_referensi_barang:'',kode_barang:sourceCode,nama_referensi:'',kandidat:candidates }; return { status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score:Math.round(best._score*100), id_referensi_barang:best.kode_barang, kode_barang:best.kode_barang, nama_referensi:best.rincian_obyek, kandidat:candidates, match_type:best._score>=0.88?'AUTO_NAME_HIGH':'AUTO_NAME' }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 7. SATU BARIS RKAS → HASIL MAPPING * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function mapOneRkasRow_(row, context) { const fastCtx = context && context.fastCtx ? context.fastCtx : _rkasBuildFastMappingContext_(); const kegiatan = mapRkasKegiatan_(row, fastCtx); let barang = mapRkasBarang_(row, kegiatan, fastCtx); // CANONICAL REFERENCE OVERRIDE: exact code in referensi_barang wins. // This mapping is independent from the activity mapping. const refIndex = fastCtx.refIndex || getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const exactRef = resolveRkasReference_(row, refIndex); if (exactRef) { barang = Object.assign({}, barang || {}, { status: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, score: 100, id_referensi_barang: exactRef.kode_barang, kode_barang: exactRef.kode_barang, rincian_obyek: exactRef.rincian_obyek, nama_kelompok_belanja: exactRef.nama_kelompok_belanja, kandidat: [exactRef], match_type: 'EXACT_CODE' }); } let status = RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED; let score = Math.min(kegiatan.score || 0, barang.score || 0); let catatan = []; if (kegiatan.status === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED || barang.status === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED) { status = RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED; } else if (kegiatan.status === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW || barang.status === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW) { status = RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW; } if (kegiatan.status !== RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED) { catatan.push('Kegiatan: ' + kegiatan.status); } if (barang.status !== RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED) { catatan.push('Belanja: ' + barang.status); } const npsn = normalizeNpsn_( context.npsn || row.npsn ); return { // Identitas sumber. id_rkas: text(row.id_rkas) || id('RKAS'), npsn: npsn, id_import: text(context.id_import), // Hasil mapping. id_kegiatan: kegiatan.id_kegiatan || '', kode_kegiatan: kegiatan.kode_kegiatan || '', id_referensi_barang: barang.id_referensi_barang || '', // Data asli RKAS. kode_rekening: normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_rekening || row.kode_barang), nama_belanja_asli: text(row.nama_belanja || row.nama_belanja_asli || row.uraian), uraian: text(row.uraian || row.nama_belanja || row.nama_belanja_asli), volume: normalizeRkasNumber_(row.volume), satuan: text(row.satuan), harga_satuan: normalizeRkasNumber_(row.harga_satuan), pagu: normalizeRkasNumber_(row.pagu), // Metadata. status_rkas: text(row.status_rkas || 'DRAFT'), tanggal_pengesahan: row.tanggal_pengesahan || '', sumber_data: text(row.sumber_data || 'UPLOAD_RKAS'), file_rkas_id: text(context.file_id || row.file_rkas_id), nomor_baris_sumber: row.nomor_baris_sumber || row.source_row || '', status_mapping: status, catatan_mapping: catatan.join('; ') || '', update_at: new Date(), // Informasi preview; tidak disimpan ke master_rkas. mapping_score: score, mapping_kegiatan: kegiatan, mapping_barang: barang }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 8. VALIDASI IMPORT * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function validateRkasImportRows_(rows, context) { if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) { throw new Error('Tidak ada baris RKAS yang dapat diproses.'); } const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(context.npsn); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN wajib diisi.'); const bad = []; rows.forEach((x, i) => { if (!text(x.kode_rekening)) bad.push('Baris ' + (i + 1) + ': kode rekening kosong.'); if (!text(x.uraian) && !text(x.nama_belanja)) bad.push('Baris ' + (i + 1) + ': uraian/nama belanja kosong.'); if (normalizeRkasNumber_(x.pagu) < 0) bad.push('Baris ' + (i + 1) + ': pagu negatif.'); }); if (bad.length) { throw new Error('Validasi RKAS gagal:\n' + bad.slice(0, 20).join('\n')); } return true; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 9. BUAT RECORD rkas_import * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function createRkasImport_(s, payload) { const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn || s.npsn || s.username); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan wajib diisi.'); const importId = id('IMP-RKAS'); insert(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT, { id_import: importId, npsn: npsn, nama_file: text(payload.nama_file), jenis_file: upper(payload.jenis_file || 'XLSX'), tahun: Number(payload.tahun || new Date().getFullYear()), versi_rkas: text(payload.versi_rkas || 'V1'), tanggal_upload: payload.tanggal_upload ? dateNorm(payload.tanggal_upload) : new Date(), uploaded_by: text(s.username || s.id), file_id: text(payload.file_id), status_import: 'PARSING', total_baris: 0, berhasil_mapping: 0, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: 0, created_at: new Date() }); return importId; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 10. PREVIEW IMPORT * * Payload minimal: * { * nama_file, * jenis_file, * tahun, * versi_rkas, * file_id, * rows: [ * { * kode_kegiatan, * kegiatan, * kode_rekening, * nama_belanja, * uraian, * volume, * satuan, * harga_satuan, * pagu, * nomor_baris_sumber * } * ] * } * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function importRKASPreview(t, payload) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload = payload || {}; // SEKOLAH hanya boleh mengimpor untuk dirinya sendiri. DINAS dapat memilih sekolah tujuan. const sessionNpsn = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); const npsn = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS ? normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn) : sessionNpsn; if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan wajib dipilih.'); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH && payload.npsn && normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn) !== sessionNpsn) { throw new Error('NPSN file tidak sesuai dengan akun sekolah.'); } let rows = Array.isArray(payload.rows) ? payload.rows : []; // Jangan pernah menyimpan subtotal meskipun frontend mengirimkannya. rows = removeRkasSubtotalRows_(rows); if (!rows.length) throw new Error('Tidak ada item belanja setelah subtotal RKAS disaring.'); validateRkasImportRows_(rows, { npsn: npsn }); payload.npsn = npsn; const importId = createRkasImport_(s, payload); // INDEX SEKALI PER IMPORT: referensi_barang dan maping_kegiatan tidak // dibaca/di-scan ulang untuk setiap baris. Ini sangat mengurangi waktu // untuk file 10.000–50.000+ detail. const fastCtx = _rkasBuildFastMappingContext_(); const mapped = rows.map((row, i) => { const source = Object.assign({}, row, { nomor_baris_sumber: row.nomor_baris_sumber || i + 1 }); return mapOneRkasRow_(source, { npsn: npsn, id_import: importId, file_id: payload.file_id, fastCtx: fastCtx }); }); const matched = mapped.filter(x => x.status_mapping === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED).length; const review = mapped.filter(x => x.status_mapping === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW).length; const unmatched = mapped.filter(x => x.status_mapping === RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED).length; updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { status_import: 'MAPPING_SELESAI', total_baris: mapped.length, berhasil_mapping: matched, perlu_konfirmasi: review, gagal_mapping: unmatched }); return { success: true, id_import: importId, npsn: npsn, summary: { total_baris: mapped.length, berhasil_mapping: matched, perlu_konfirmasi: review, gagal_mapping: unmatched }, data: mapped }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 11. UPDATE STATUS rkas_import * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, patch) { const sh = sheet(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT); const h = headers(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT); const idCol = h.indexOf('id_import') + 1; if (idCol < 1) throw new Error('Kolom id_import pada rkas_import tidak ditemukan.'); const values = sh.getDataRange().getValues(); for (let r = 1; r < values.length; r++) { if (text(values[r][idCol - 1]) === text(importId)) { Object.keys(patch || {}).forEach(k => { const c = h.indexOf(k) + 1; if (c > 0) sh.getRange(r + 1, c).setValue(patch[k]); }); return true; } } throw new Error('Data import tidak ditemukan: ' + importId); } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 12. KONFIRMASI MANUAL * * selections: * [ * { * nomor_baris_sumber: 27, * id_referensi_barang: "5.1.02....", * id_kegiatan: "....", * catatan_mapping: "Dipilih sekolah" * } * ] * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function confirmRKASMapping(t, payload) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload = payload || {}; const rows = Array.isArray(payload.rows) ? payload.rows : []; if (!rows.length) throw new Error('Tidak ada mapping yang dikonfirmasi.'); const npsn = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS ? normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn) : normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan wajib diisi.'); return { success: true, npsn: npsn, id_import: text(payload.id_import), data: rows.map(x => ({ nomor_baris_sumber: x.nomor_baris_sumber, id_kegiatan: text(x.id_kegiatan), id_referensi_barang: text(x.id_referensi_barang), status_mapping: RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MANUAL, catatan_mapping: text(x.catatan_mapping || 'Konfirmasi manual sekolah') })) }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 13. COMMIT KE master_rkas * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function ensureRkasSchemaV142_() { const ss = db(); const schema = { [SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS]: EXPECTED_HEADERS.master_rkas, [SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT]: EXPECTED_HEADERS.rkas_import }; const report = {}; Object.keys(schema).forEach(name => { let sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(name); sh.getRange(1,1,1,schema[name].length).setValues([schema[name]]); report[name] = {created:true, added:schema[name].slice()}; return; } let h = headers(name); const added = []; schema[name].forEach(col => { if (!h.includes(col)) { sh.getRange(1, sh.getLastColumn()+1).setValue(col); added.push(col); h.push(col); } }); report[name] = {created:false, added:added}; }); return report; } function _findRkasImport_(importId) { return records(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT).find(x => text(x.id_import) === text(importId)) || null; } function _countMasterRkasByImport_(importId, npsn) { const wanted = normalizeNpsn_(npsn); const rows = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).filter(x => text(x.id_import) === text(importId) && (!wanted || normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === wanted) ); return {count: rows.length, rows: rows}; } function commitRKASImport(t, payload) { const lock = LockService.getScriptLock(); if (!lock.tryLock(30000)) throw new Error('Sistem sedang memproses import RKAS lain. Silakan tunggu beberapa detik lalu coba lagi.'); try { return _commitRKASImportCore_(t, payload); } finally { try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (_) {} } } function _rkasRowFingerprint_(x) { const desc = _rkasSubtotalDescription_(x && (x.uraian || x.nama_belanja_asli || x.nama_belanja || '')); const code = normalizeRkasCode_(x && x.kode_rekening); const volume = normalizeRkasNumber_(x && x.volume); const satuan = normalizeRkasText_(x && x.satuan); const harga = normalizeRkasNumber_(x && x.harga_satuan); const pagu = normalizeRkasNumber_(x && x.pagu); return [code, desc, volume, satuan, harga, pagu].join('|'); } function _rkasFingerprintCounts_(rows) { const out = {}; (rows || []).forEach(x => { const k = _rkasRowFingerprint_(x); if (k) out[k] = (out[k] || 0) + 1; }); return out; } function _sameRkasFingerprintSet_(a, b) { const A = _rkasFingerprintCounts_(a), B = _rkasFingerprintCounts_(b); const ak = Object.keys(A), bk = Object.keys(B); if (ak.length !== bk.length) return false; for (const k of ak) if (A[k] !== B[k]) return false; return true; } function _findIdenticalApprovedImport_(npsn, incomingRows, excludeImportId) { const all = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const groups = {}; const n = normalizeNpsn_(npsn); all.forEach(row => { if (normalizeNpsn_(row.npsn) !== n) return; if (upper(row.status_rkas) !== 'APPROVED') return; if (text(row.id_import) === text(excludeImportId)) return; const iid = text(row.id_import); if (!iid) return; // legacy tanpa id_import tidak dapat dijadikan referensi paket baru if (!groups[iid]) groups[iid] = []; groups[iid].push(row); }); for (const iid of Object.keys(groups)) { const rows = groups[iid]; if (rows.length !== (incomingRows || []).length) continue; if (_sameRkasFingerprintSet_(rows, incomingRows)) { return { id_import: iid, rows: rows }; } } return null; } function _rkasResolveReferenceId_(imp) { return text(imp && imp.id_import_referensi); } function _rkasDetailRowsForImport_(allMaster, importId, allImports) { const direct = (allMaster || []).filter(x => text(x.id_import) === text(importId)); const imp = (allImports || []).find(x => text(x.id_import) === text(importId)); const ref = _rkasResolveReferenceId_(imp); // Jika import merupakan salinan identik, id_import_referensi adalah sumber // detail yang sah. Jangan biarkan satu baris marker/placeholder pada import // baru (mis. pagu 0) menutupi 34 baris RKAS asli yang sudah APPROVED. if (ref) { const refRows = (allMaster || []).filter(x => text(x.id_import) === ref); const refTotal = refRows.reduce((sum, x) => sum + num(x.pagu), 0); if (refRows.length && refTotal > 0) { const refIndex = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const enriched = refRows.map(row => enrichBelanjaMapping(row, refIndex)); return { rows: enriched, resolvedImportId: ref, referenced: true }; } } if (direct.length) { const refIndex = getRkasReferenceIndex_(); const enriched = direct.map(row => enrichBelanjaMapping(row, refIndex)); return { rows: enriched, resolvedImportId: text(importId), referenced: false }; } return { rows: [], resolvedImportId: text(importId), referenced: false }; } function _commitRKASImportCore_(t, payload) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload = payload || {}; ensureRkasSchemaV142_(); const importId = text(payload.id_import); let rows = Array.isArray(payload.rows) ? payload.rows.filter(x => x && typeof x === 'object') : []; // Defense-in-depth: subtotal tidak boleh masuk master_rkas walaupun request // datang langsung dari client atau merupakan retry dari frontend lama. rows = removeRkasSubtotalRows_(rows); if (!importId) throw new Error('ID import RKAS wajib diisi.'); if (!rows.length) throw new Error('Tidak ada data RKAS yang dicentang untuk disimpan.'); const imp = _findRkasImport_(importId); if (!imp) throw new Error('Import RKAS tidak ditemukan: ' + importId); // Sumber NPSN harus tunggal dan deterministik. const sessionNpsn = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); const payloadNpsn = normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn); const importNpsn = normalizeNpsn_(imp.npsn); let npsn = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS ? (payloadNpsn || importNpsn) : sessionNpsn; if (!npsn) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan tidak ditemukan. Pilih sekolah tujuan terlebih dahulu.'); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH && npsn !== sessionNpsn) { throw new Error('Import RKAS bukan milik sekolah yang sedang login.'); } if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS && importNpsn && payloadNpsn && importNpsn !== payloadNpsn) { throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan berubah antara preview dan simpan. Silakan pilih sekolah tujuan lalu impor ulang.'); } // Pastikan sekolah tujuan memang terdaftar. const school = records(SHEETS.SEKOLAH).find(x => normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn) === npsn); if (!school) throw new Error('NPSN sekolah tujuan ' + npsn + ' tidak ditemukan pada master sekolah.'); // Hanya status mapping yang sah yang masuk; baris yang dicentang tetap boleh // disimpan sebagai MANUAL tanpa memaksa user memahami mapping teknis. rows.forEach(x => { const st = upper(x.status_mapping); if (![RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED, RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MANUAL].includes(st)) { x.status_mapping = RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MANUAL; x.catatan_mapping = text(x.catatan_mapping || 'Dipilih pengguna saat impor RKAS'); } }); const existing = _countMasterRkasByImport_(importId, npsn); // ANTI-DUPLIKASI LINTAS IMPORT: bila seluruh item yang akan disimpan // identik dengan satu paket APPROVED yang sudah aktif, jangan membuat // baris master_rkas kedua. Paket import baru menjadi referensi ke paket // APPROVED yang sudah ada dan tetap tercatat sebagai histori import. if (existing.count === 0) { const identical = _findIdenticalApprovedImport_(npsn, rows, importId); if (identical) { const duplicateTotal = identical.rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+num(x.pagu),0); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { npsn: npsn, status_import: 'APPROVED', id_import_referensi: identical.id_import, total_baris: identical.rows.length, berhasil_mapping: identical.rows.length, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: 0 }); invalidateRkasCache_(npsn); return { success:true, message:'RKAS identik sudah APPROVED sebelumnya. Data tidak digandakan; import baru ditautkan ke RKAS yang sudah disetujui.', id_import:importId, id_import_referensi:identical.id_import, npsn:npsn, jumlah_baris:identical.rows.length, total_pagu:duplicateTotal, status_import:'APPROVED', status_rkas:'APPROVED', alreadySaved:true, duplicate:true, verified:true }; } } if (existing.count > 0) { // Commit idempoten: jangan menggandakan data jika browser melakukan retry. const expectedStatus = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS ? 'APPROVED' : 'DIAJUKAN'; const statusCol = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).indexOf('status_rkas') + 1; const updateCol = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS).indexOf('update_at') + 1; const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const idCol = h.indexOf('id_import') + 1, nCol = h.indexOf('npsn') + 1; const now = new Date(); for (let r=2; r<=sh.getLastRow(); r++) { if (text(sh.getRange(r,idCol).getValue())===importId && normalizeNpsn_(sh.getRange(r,nCol).getValue())===npsn) { if (statusCol>0) sh.getRange(r,statusCol).setValue(expectedStatus); if (updateCol>0) sh.getRange(r,updateCol).setValue(now); } } updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { status_import: expectedStatus, total_baris: existing.count, berhasil_mapping: existing.count, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: 0 }); invalidateRkasCache_(npsn); return { success:true, message:'RKAS sudah tersimpan; sistem tidak menggandakan data.', id_import:importId, npsn:npsn, jumlah_baris:existing.count, total_pagu:existing.rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+num(x.pagu),0), status_import:expectedStatus, status_rkas:expectedStatus, alreadySaved:true, verified:true }; } const sh = sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const h = headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const directApproved = s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS; const commitStatus = directApproved ? 'APPROVED' : 'DIAJUKAN'; const commitSource = directApproved ? 'DINAS_UPLOAD_RKAS' : 'SEKOLAH_UPLOAD_RKAS'; const approvalDate = directApproved ? new Date() : ''; const now = new Date(); const output = rows.map(x => { const d = { id_rkas: text(x.id_rkas) || id('RKAS'), npsn: npsn, versi_rkas: text(x.versi_rkas || imp.versi_rkas || ''), id_import: importId, kode_rekening: normalizeRkasCode_(x.kode_rekening), id_referensi_barang: text(x.id_referensi_barang), nama_belanja_asli: text(x.nama_belanja_asli || x.nama_belanja || x.uraian), uraian: text(x.uraian || x.nama_belanja_asli || x.nama_belanja), volume: normalizeRkasNumber_(x.volume), satuan: text(x.satuan), harga_satuan: normalizeRkasNumber_(x.harga_satuan), pagu: normalizeRkasNumber_(x.pagu), status_rkas: commitStatus, tanggal_pengesahan: approvalDate, sumber_data: commitSource, file_rkas_id: text(x.file_rkas_id || imp.file_id), nomor_baris_sumber: x.nomor_baris_sumber || '', status_mapping: upper(x.status_mapping || RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MANUAL), catatan_mapping: text(x.catatan_mapping), update_at: now }; return h.map(k => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d,k) ? d[k] : ''); }); const firstRow = sh.getLastRow() + 1; sh.getRange(firstRow, 1, output.length, h.length).setValues(output); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); // VERIFIKASI WAJIB: data yang baru ditulis harus benar-benar ditemukan kembali. const verify = _countMasterRkasByImport_(importId, npsn); if (verify.count !== output.length) { throw new Error('Commit RKAS gagal diverifikasi. Diminta menyimpan ' + output.length + ' baris, tetapi master_rkas hanya menemukan ' + verify.count + ' baris untuk NPSN ' + npsn + ' dan id_import ' + importId + '.'); } const totalPagu = verify.rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+num(x.pagu),0); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId, { npsn: npsn, status_import: commitStatus, total_baris: verify.count, berhasil_mapping: verify.count, perlu_konfirmasi: 0, gagal_mapping: 0 }); invalidateRkasCache_(npsn); return { success:true, message:'RKAS berhasil disimpan ke master_rkas dan telah diverifikasi.', id_import:importId, npsn:npsn, jumlah_baris:verify.count, total_pagu:totalPagu, status_import:commitStatus, status_rkas:commitStatus, verified:true }; } /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- * 14. OPTIONAL: AMBIL HASIL PREVIEW / IMPORT * -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */ function getRKASImport(t, idImport) { const s = requireRole(t, [CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH, CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); const requested = text(idImport); if (!requested) throw new Error('ID import RKAS wajib diisi.'); const imports = records(SHEETS.RKAS_IMPORT).filter(x => text(x.id_import) === requested); if (!imports.length) throw new Error('Import RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); const imp = imports[0]; const npsn = normalizeNpsn_(s.npsn || s.username); if (s.role === CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH && normalizeNpsn_(imp.npsn) !== npsn) { throw new Error('Anda tidak memiliki akses ke import RKAS ini.'); } const allMaster = records(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS); const resolved = _rkasDetailRowsForImport_(allMaster, requested, imports); const rows = resolved.rows; const rowStatuses = rows.map(x => upper(x.status_rkas)).filter(Boolean); const importStatus = upper(imp.status_import || ''); let effectiveStatus = importStatus; if (importStatus === 'APPROVED') { effectiveStatus = 'APPROVED'; } else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'APPROVED')) { effectiveStatus = 'APPROVED'; } else if (!['APPROVED','DITOLAK','PERLU_PERBAIKAN','DIAJUKAN'].includes(importStatus)) { if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'DITOLAK')) effectiveStatus = 'DITOLAK'; else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN')) effectiveStatus = 'PERLU_PERBAIKAN'; else if (rowStatuses.length && rowStatuses.every(x => x === 'DIAJUKAN')) effectiveStatus = 'DIAJUKAN'; else effectiveStatus = importStatus || 'DRAFT'; } if (upper(imp.status_import) !== effectiveStatus && ['APPROVED','DITOLAK','PERLU_PERBAIKAN','DIAJUKAN'].includes(effectiveStatus)) { try { updateRkasImportStatus_(requested, {status_import: effectiveStatus}); imp.status_import = effectiveStatus; } catch (_) {} } return { success: true, import: imp, data: rows, summary: { id_import: requested, resolved_import_id: resolved.resolvedImportId, referenced: resolved.referenced, npsn: normalizeNpsn_(imp.npsn), status_import: effectiveStatus, total_baris: rows.length || Number(imp.total_baris || 0), total_pagu: rows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+num(x.pagu),0) || Number(imp.total_pagu || 0) } }; } /* ============================================================================ * 16. CONTOH PAYLOAD DARI FRONTEND * ============================================================================ * * api('importRKASPreview', { * data: { * nama_file: file.name, * jenis_file: 'XLSX', * tahun: 2026, * versi_rkas: 'V1', * file_id: uploadedFileId, * rows: parsedRows * } * }); * * parsedRows: * [ * { * kode_kegiatan: '...', * kegiatan: 'Pelaksanaan Pendaftaran Murid Baru (PMB)', * kode_rekening: '5.1.02.01.01.0024', * nama_belanja: 'Ballpoint Standard AE7', * uraian: 'Ballpoint Standard AE7', * volume: 20, * satuan: 'buah', * harga_satuan: 3500, * pagu: 70000, * nomor_baris_sumber: 27 * } * ] * * Hasil preview dikirim ke frontend. Baris REVIEW/UNMATCHED diselesaikan * sekolah. Setelah itu frontend mengirim: * * api('commitRKASImport', { * data: { * id_import: 'IMP-RKAS-...', * rows: resolvedRows * } * }); * ========================================================================= */ /* ============================================================================ * RKAS REPLACE / MULTI-FORMAT IMPORT ENGINE — FINAL20 * ========================================================================== */ const RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_SHEET_='rkas_import_detail'; const RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_HEADERS_=[ 'id_import','npsn','versi_rkas','kode_kegiatan','id_kegiatan','nama_kegiatan_asli', 'kode_rekening','id_referensi_barang','nama_belanja_asli','uraian','volume','satuan', 'harga_satuan','pagu','nomor_baris_sumber','status_mapping','catatan_mapping', 'status_rkas','created_at' ]; function _ensureRkasReplaceDetailSheet_(){ const ss=db(); let sh=ss.getSheetByName(RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_SHEET_); if(!sh){ sh=ss.insertSheet(RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_SHEET_); sh.getRange(1,1,1,RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_HEADERS_.length).setValues([RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_HEADERS_]); }else{ const current=sh.getRange(1,1,Math.max(1,sh.getLastColumn())).getValues()[0].map(String); const missing=RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_HEADERS_.filter(h=>!current.includes(h)); if(missing.length) sh.getRange(1,sh.getLastColumn()+1,1,missing.length).setValues([missing]); } return sh; } function _normalizeReplaceScope_(v){ const x=upper(v||'ONE'); return (x==='ALL'||x==='SELURUH'||x==='SELURUH_SEKOLAH')?'ALL':'ONE'; } function _validateOfficialNpsn_(n){ n=normalizeNpsn_(n); return n&&getSekolahMap()[n]?n:null; } function rkasReplaceStart(t,payload){ const lock=LockService.getScriptLock(); if(!lock.tryLock(15000)) throw new Error('Sistem sedang memproses penggantian RKAS lain. Silakan tunggu beberapa detik.'); try{ const s=requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload=payload||{}; const scope=_normalizeReplaceScope_(payload.scope), target=normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn); if(scope==='ONE'&&!_validateOfficialNpsn_(target)) throw new Error('Sekolah tujuan tidak valid. Pilih sekolah dari master sekolah.'); const tahun=Number(payload.tahun||new Date().getFullYear()); const versi=text(payload.versi_rkas||String(tahun)); if(tahun<2000||tahun>2100) throw new Error('Tahun anggaran tidak valid.'); const importId=createRkasImport_(s,{ npsn:scope==='ALL'?'MULTI':target,nama_file:text(payload.nama_file), jenis_file:upper(payload.jenis_file||'XLSX'),tahun,versi_rkas:versi,file_id:text(payload.file_id) }); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId,{status_import:'REPLACE_PARSING',total_baris:0,berhasil_mapping:0,perlu_konfirmasi:0,gagal_mapping:0}); _ensureRkasReplaceDetailSheet_(); PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('RKAS_REPLACE_'+importId,JSON.stringify({accepted:0,matched:0,review:0,unmatched:0,skipped:0,updated_at:Date.now()})); return {success:true,id_import:importId,scope,npsn:scope==='ONE'?target:'',tahun,versi_rkas:versi}; } finally { try{lock.releaseLock();}catch(_){} } } function _rkasReplaceDetailRows_(importId){ const sh=_ensureRkasReplaceDetailSheet_(),h=headers(RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_SHEET_); const vals=sh.getDataRange().getValues(),out=[]; for(let r=1;ro[k]=vals[r][c]);out.push(o); } return out; } function _rkasReplaceProgress_(importId,delta){ const props=PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(),key='RKAS_REPLACE_'+importId; let cur={accepted:0,matched:0,review:0,unmatched:0,skipped:0}; try{cur=Object.assign(cur,JSON.parse(props.getProperty(key)||'{}'));}catch(_){ } Object.keys(delta||{}).forEach(k=>cur[k]=(Number(cur[k])||0)+(Number(delta[k])||0)); cur.updated_at=Date.now(); props.setProperty(key,JSON.stringify(cur)); return cur; } /* ============================================================================ * RKAS IMPORT MULTI-FORMAT — FINAL20 * Server-side guard for both supported Excel shapes. * ========================================================================== */ function _rkasRowType_(row){ const t=upper(row && (row.row_type || row.jenis_baris || row.tipe_baris || '')); if(['SUBTOTAL','PARENT','HEADER','GROUP','REKENING','TOTAL'].indexOf(t)>=0) return t; if(row && (row.is_subtotal===true || String(row.is_subtotal).toLowerCase()==='true')) return 'SUBTOTAL'; return ''; } function _looksLikeRkasParent_(row){ if(!row) return false; if(_rkasRowType_(row)) return true; const uraian=text(row.uraian||row.nama_belanja||row.nama_belanja_asli||''); const vol=normalizeRkasNumber_(row.volume); const harga=normalizeRkasNumber_(row.harga_satuan); return /^belanja\b/i.test(uraian) && !(vol>0) && !(harga>0); } function _rkasMappingContext_(){ const kegiatan=getRkasMapingKegiatanRows_(), byCode={}; kegiatan.forEach(x=>{ const k=normalizeRkasCode_(x.kode_kegiatan); if(k && !byCode[k]) byCode[k]=x; }); return {kegiatanByCode:byCode, refIndex:getRkasReferenceIndex_()}; } function _mapRkasKegiatanFast_(row,ctx){ const sourceCode=normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_kegiatan||row.kode_program||''); if(sourceCode && ctx.kegiatanByCode[sourceCode]){ const x=ctx.kegiatanByCode[sourceCode]; return {status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED,score:100,id_kegiatan:x.kode_kegiatan||'', kode_kegiatan:x.kode_kegiatan||sourceCode, kegiatan:x.uraian||x.sub_program||x.program,kandidat:[x]}; } return mapRkasKegiatan_(row); } function _resolveRkasBarangFast_(row,ctx){ const exact=resolveRkasReference_({ kode_rekening:normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_rekening||row.kode_barang), id_referensi_barang:row.id_referensi_barang },ctx.refIndex); if(exact){ return {status:RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED,score:100,id_referensi_barang:exact.kode_barang, kode_barang:exact.kode_barang,nama_referensi:exact.rincian_obyek, rincian_obyek:exact.rincian_obyek,kandidat:[exact],match_type:'EXACT_CODE'}; } return mapRkasBarang_(row,null); } function rkasReplaceAppend(t,payload){ const lock=LockService.getScriptLock(); if(!lock.tryLock(20000)) throw new Error('Sistem sedang menulis batch RKAS lain. Silakan tunggu.'); try{ const s=requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]); payload=payload||{}; const importId=text(payload.id_import),rows=Array.isArray(payload.rows)?payload.rows:[]; if(!importId) throw new Error('ID replace RKAS wajib diisi.'); const imp=_findRkasImport_(importId); if(!imp) throw new Error('Sesi replace RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); if(!/^REPLACE_/.test(upper(imp.status_import))) throw new Error('Sesi replace RKAS sudah ditutup.'); const scope=_normalizeReplaceScope_(payload.scope),target=normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn); const ctx=_rkasMappingContext_(); const out=[],skipped=[],skipReasons=[]; let matched=0,review=0,unmatched=0; rows.forEach((raw,idx)=>{ const row=raw||{}; if(_looksLikeRkasParent_(row)){ skipped.push(idx+1); skipReasons.push('SUBTOTAL/PARENT'); return; } const npsn=normalizeNpsn_(row.npsn||target); if(!_validateOfficialNpsn_(npsn)){ skipped.push(npsn); skipReasons.push('NPSN_TIDAK_RESMI'); return; } if(scope==='ONE' && npsn!==target) throw new Error('File memuat NPSN '+npsn+', bukan sekolah tujuan '+target+'.'); const kode=normalizeRkasCode_(row.kode_rekening||row.kode_barang); const uraian=text(row.uraian||row.nama_belanja||row.nama_belanja_asli); const pagu=normalizeRkasNumber_(row.pagu); if(!kode || !uraian || !(pagu>0)){ skipped.push(idx+1); skipReasons.push('BARIS_TIDAK_LENGKAP'); return; } const ref=_resolveRkasBarangFast_(row,ctx); const kegiatan=_mapRkasKegiatanFast_(row,ctx); const notes=[]; if(!ref) notes.push('Referensi barang belum ditemukan exact-code'); if(kegiatan.status!==RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED) notes.push('Kegiatan: '+kegiatan.status); const st=ref ? RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED : (kegiatan.status===RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED ? RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW : RKAS_MAP_STATUS.UNMATCHED); if(st===RKAS_MAP_STATUS.MATCHED) matched++; else if(st===RKAS_MAP_STATUS.REVIEW) review++; else unmatched++; out.push([ importId,npsn,text(imp.versi_rkas||row.versi_rkas||''), text(row.kode_kegiatan||kegiatan.kode_kegiatan||''), text(row.id_kegiatan||kegiatan.id_kegiatan||''), text(row.nama_kegiatan_asli||row.kegiatan||''), kode,ref?ref.kode_barang:'', text(row.nama_belanja_asli||uraian),uraian, normalizeRkasNumber_(row.volume)||1,text(row.satuan||'paket'), normalizeRkasNumber_(row.harga_satuan)||pagu,pagu, Number(row.nomor_baris_sumber||idx+1),st,notes.join('; '), 'IMPORT_PENDING',new Date() ]); }); if(out.length){ const sh=_ensureRkasReplaceDetailSheet_(); sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow()+1,1,out.length,RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_HEADERS_.length).setValues(out); } const prog=_rkasReplaceProgress_(importId,{ accepted:out.length,matched,review,unmatched,skipped:skipped.length }); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId,{ status_import:'REPLACE_STAGED',total_baris:prog.accepted, berhasil_mapping:prog.matched,perlu_konfirmasi:prog.review,gagal_mapping:prog.unmatched }); return { success:true,id_import:importId,accepted:out.length,skipped:skipped.length, skipped_rows:skipped.slice(0,100),skip_reasons:[...new Set(skipReasons)], total_baris:prog.accepted,berhasil_mapping:prog.matched, perlu_konfirmasi:prog.review,gagal_mapping:prog.unmatched }; } finally { try{lock.releaseLock();}catch(_){} } } function _transactionRkasRefIndexForSchools_(npsns){ const wanted={};(npsns||[]).forEach(n=>{n=normalizeNpsn_(n);if(n)wanted[n]=true;}); const refs={};Object.keys(wanted).forEach(n=>refs[n]={}); try{ records(SHEETS.TRANSAKSI_SPJ).forEach(x=>{ const n=normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn),idR=text(x.id_rkas); if(n&&wanted[n]&&idR)refs[n][idR]=true; }); }catch(_){ } return refs; } function _deleteOrArchiveOldRkasForReplace_(npsns,keepImportId){ const targets={};(npsns||[]).forEach(n=>{if(n)targets[normalizeNpsn_(n)]=true;}); const sh=sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS),h=headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS),data=sh.getDataRange().getValues(); if(data.length<2)return {deleted:0,archived:0}; const idxN=h.indexOf('npsn'),idxId=h.indexOf('id_rkas'),idxImp=h.indexOf('id_import'); const idxStatus=h.indexOf('status_rkas'),idxUpdate=h.indexOf('update_at'); const refs=_transactionRkasRefIndexForSchools_(Object.keys(targets)); const deleteRows=[],archiveRows=[],now=new Date(); for(let i=1;i=0&&text(data[i][idxImp])===text(keepImportId))continue; const idr=idxId>=0?text(data[i][idxId]):''; if(idr&&refs[n]&&refs[n][idr]) archiveRows.push(i); else deleteRows.push(i+1); if(archiveRows.length && archiveRows[archiveRows.length-1]===i && idxStatus>=0) data[i][idxStatus]='ARCHIVED_REPLACED'; if(archiveRows.length && archiveRows[archiveRows.length-1]===i && idxUpdate>=0) data[i][idxUpdate]=now; } // Tulis status archive sekaligus; tidak ada setValue per baris. if(archiveRows.length){ sh.getRange(1,1,data.length,h.length).setValues(data); } deleteRows.sort((a,b)=>a-b); const blocks=[];let start=null,prev=null; deleteRows.forEach(r=>{if(start===null){start=prev=r;}else if(r===prev+1)prev=r;else{blocks.push([start,prev-start+1]);start=prev=r;}}); if(start!==null)blocks.push([start,prev-start+1]); blocks.reverse().forEach(([r,count])=>sh.deleteRows(r,count)); return {deleted:deleteRows.length,archived:archiveRows.length}; } function _cleanupRkasReplaceStaging_(importId){ const sh=_ensureRkasReplaceDetailSheet_(),h=headers(RKAS_REPLACE_DETAIL_SHEET_),c=h.indexOf('id_import')+1; if(c<1||sh.getLastRow()<2)return; const vals=sh.getDataRange().getValues(),del=[]; for(let r=1;ra-b); const blocks=[];let start=null,prev=null; del.forEach(r=>{if(start===null){start=prev=r;}else if(r===prev+1)prev=r;else{blocks.push([start,prev-start+1]);start=prev=r;}}); if(start!==null)blocks.push([start,prev-start+1]); blocks.reverse().forEach(([r,count])=>sh.deleteRows(r,count)); } function rkasReplaceFinalize(t,payload){ const lock=LockService.getScriptLock(); if(!lock.tryLock(30000)) throw new Error('Sistem sedang memproses penggantian RKAS lain. Silakan tunggu.'); try{ const s=requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS]);payload=payload||{}; const importId=text(payload.id_import);if(!importId)throw new Error('ID replace RKAS wajib diisi.'); const imp=_findRkasImport_(importId);if(!imp)throw new Error('Sesi replace RKAS tidak ditemukan.'); const rows=_rkasReplaceDetailRows_(importId);if(!rows.length)throw new Error('Tidak ada detail RKAS pada staging.'); const scope=_normalizeReplaceScope_(payload.scope),target=normalizeNpsn_(payload.npsn); const npsns=[...new Set(rows.map(x=>normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn)).filter(Boolean))]; if(scope==='ONE'&&(npsns.length!==1||npsns[0]!==target))throw new Error('Mode satu sekolah harus menghasilkan tepat satu NPSN.'); const invalid=npsns.filter(n=>!_validateOfficialNpsn_(n));if(invalid.length)throw new Error('NPSN tidak resmi: '+invalid.join(', ')); const total=rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+num(x.pagu),0);if(!(total>0))throw new Error('Total pagu harus lebih dari nol.'); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId,{status_import:'REPLACE_WRITING',total_baris:rows.length}); const sh=sheet(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS),h=headers(SHEETS.MASTER_RKAS),now=new Date(),out=[]; rows.forEach((x,i)=>{ const d={ id_rkas:id('RKAS'),npsn:normalizeNpsn_(x.npsn),versi_rkas:text(x.versi_rkas||imp.versi_rkas||''), id_import:importId,kode_kegiatan:text(x.kode_kegiatan),id_kegiatan:text(x.id_kegiatan), nama_kegiatan_asli:text(x.nama_kegiatan_asli),kode_rekening:normalizeRkasCode_(x.kode_rekening), id_referensi_barang:text(x.id_referensi_barang),nama_belanja_asli:text(x.nama_belanja_asli||x.uraian), uraian:text(x.uraian||x.nama_belanja_asli),volume:normalizeRkasNumber_(x.volume),satuan:text(x.satuan), harga_satuan:normalizeRkasNumber_(x.harga_satuan),pagu:normalizeRkasNumber_(x.pagu), status_rkas:'APPROVED',tanggal_pengesahan:now,sumber_data:'DINAS_REPLACE_RKAS', file_rkas_id:text(imp.file_id),nomor_baris_sumber:x.nomor_baris_sumber||i+1, status_mapping:upper(x.status_mapping||'UNMATCHED'),catatan_mapping:text(x.catatan_mapping),update_at:now }; out.push(h.map(k=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(d,k)?d[k]:'')); }); const CHUNK=2500; const firstNewRow=sh.getLastRow()+1; for(let i=0;itext(r[idCol])===importId).length; if(verified!==out.length){ sh.deleteRows(firstNewRow,out.length); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId,{status_import:'REPLACE_GAGAL'}); throw new Error('Verifikasi gagal: '+verified+' / '+out.length+' baris baru terbaca.'); } const cleanup=_deleteOrArchiveOldRkasForReplace_(npsns,importId); updateRkasImportStatus_(importId,{status_import:'APPROVED',total_baris:out.length, berhasil_mapping:rows.filter(x=>upper(x.status_mapping)==='MATCHED').length, perlu_konfirmasi:rows.filter(x=>upper(x.status_mapping)==='REVIEW').length, gagal_mapping:rows.filter(x=>upper(x.status_mapping)==='UNMATCHED').length}); _cleanupRkasReplaceStaging_(importId); PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().deleteProperty('RKAS_REPLACE_'+importId); npsns.forEach(n=>invalidateRkasCache_(n)); return {success:true,id_import:importId,scope,npsn:scope==='ONE'?npsns[0]:'', jumlah_sekolah:npsns.length,jumlah_baris:out.length,total_pagu:total,status:'APPROVED', replaced:true,deleted_lama:cleanup.deleted,archived_karena_transaksi:cleanup.archived}; } finally { try{lock.releaseLock();}catch(_){} } } function rkasMappingDiagnostic(t,payload){ requireRole(t,[CONFIG.ROLES.DINAS,CONFIG.ROLES.SEKOLAH]);payload=payload||{}; const kode=normalizeRkasCode_(payload.kode_rekening||payload.kode||'');if(!kode)throw new Error('Kode rekening wajib diisi.'); const idx=getRkasReferenceIndex_(),ref=idx.byCode[kode]||null; return {success:true,kode_rekening:kode,matched:!!ref,reference:ref, message:ref?'Exact match referensi_barang ditemukan.':'Kode tidak ditemukan di referensi_barang.'}; } // ============================================================================ // FINAL BACKEND CHECK: getRingkasanRKASRealisasi is registered in doPost. // Deployment Web App must be updated to a NEW VERSION after saving this file. // ============================================================================
